# The engine

This document describes the engine as this branch holds it (the branch atom-arranged, 2026-10-19: the engine of main at 3.0.0 with the family's sign under exchange, section 4's key `exchange`, act 6's factor and section 10's item 9, the three declared words entered on the owner's word of 2026-10-19, and the click that runs back on the owner's word of 2026-10-19, section 4's key `reach` (the key `meeting` of that day refused by name since 2026-10-20), act 6's readings in the time's order, section 10's item 11 and the module `meeting_back.py`, and the write at the birth with the births' book and the lay across parts on the owner's word of 2026-10-20, act 6, section 10's items 11 and 12; at the merge the sentence reads again as main holds it), the engine V2 at 3.0.0 (the click as one act, the spin and the atom on the V2 step, 2026-10-19): Rule3 only, written once in the law's flux form (`core/rule3.rule3`: w (a_next - 2 a_now + a_before) + r' - r = SUM over the six Links of R_ij (m_ij - a_now) - D_i a_now, the step `flux.py` gathering the Links' sum Link by Link; the owner's word of 2026-10-16, the engine matches the law in its implementation and not only in its results; the results those of 2.0.0 on the unclicked worlds and the paper's run, `tools/step_oracle.py`, the clicked worlds' readings moved where the law's dated lines say (CHANGELOG.md, 3.0.0), and a level above the amplitude bound 6,949 refused), every act on a level Rule3's carried division with the remainder kept, held so by the three gates of section 8; what changed from the tag v1.1.0 is listed in [CHANGELOG.md](../CHANGELOG.md) and, with the open items by name, in section 10. It stands beside [ALGEBRA.md](ALGEBRA.md) (the law) and [HIGHLIGHTS.md](HIGHLIGHTS.md) (the decisions in force). The paper's engine is the tag v1.1.0 (the freeze at 441b2399), run from the tag and not updated: the lines of the law entered after the freeze, the clock family on the Ports the first, are not implemented in it, and its known issues stand in section 10. The law, docs/ALGEBRA.md, and the paper are the living documents. The engine is the law's implementation and nothing else (by decision): a lattice of Nodes, every family's NodeState at every Node, and one interval of acts in the law's order, each a call of Rule3 (`src/event_universe/core/rule3.py`; Rule3, named for the three directions of the vector it works with, the Link's paces: one read coefficient per Port, R_ij = 2 num p_i^2 Q_ij on the Link to that neighbour, the product of the Node's pace squared, p_i = p_0^2 / Gamma from the Node's own content, the composed clock p_0 = Gamma (1 - 1 / Gamma)^c twice (`src/event_universe/core/paces.py`, the law's two functions of the content), and the Link's factor Q_ij, the Link's tension booked squared as one integer per Link in the unit G^2 of the run's Link unit G, read the same from both ends (ALGEBRA.md #the-paces, The clock is the Node's, the tension is the Link's); the six Ports the three directions twice; three levels in time) on whole-board arrays, every neighbour read through a Port (`src/event_universe/core/ports.py`). The engine holds no number, no formula, no family's name and no flag: every physical value comes from the run's files. No act of the interval takes a square root and no act reads a Node to decide anything: the guard reads the whole initial state once at load, as squares. Beside Rule3's acts the loop takes the NodeDetectors', from outside the Node as the lay and the receding face are: where the world declares the `draw` the NodeDetectors draw inside the run, window after window, and write their click on the lattice at one Node (`src/event_universe/credit.py`, `features/click`; HIGHLIGHTS.md, the engine, A click writes on the lattice: after a click the paths are cancelled on the lattice, keeping Rule3); the Node knows nothing of it, and a world without the key runs as before bit for bit. The repository holds the engine, the tools that lay, run, gate and read a world, and the worlds that gate the engine, the two slits and Bell, with the GHZ gate beside Bell's, the same reader over three sides (HIGHLIGHTS.md, the experiments and the worlds, **One experiment and one gate**); sections 4 to 6 are the input and the output explained with the two slits and Bell as the examples, and section 9 is the recipe for a new experiment as the shipped folders have it.

## 1. The words

- **Node**: one place of the lattice. Its whole local information is its **NodeState**, the law's own numbers and nothing else (section 2).
- **Port** and **Link**: a Node has six Ports, +x, -x, +y, -y, +z, -z; a Link is the pair of Ports between two neighbours. A level reaches a neighbour only through a Port (`arrival`). An axis is periodic (it wraps), closed (a wall, read as 0) or open (a wall read as 0 whose outer layer of `face_depth` Nodes is told to report); an axis of one layer folds, its two Ports returning the Node itself. An inner face is the same rule declared inside the board (`faces` in the world file, section 4): a plane of Nodes across an axis, beyond the board but for its gaps; a Node beyond the board is read as 0 through every Port and reads 0 through its own (`core/ports.py`, `Wrap.beyond`), so no level, current or source crosses its Links, nothing is laid or sourced there, and no body, packet or NodeDetector may stand there (refused by name). A **receding face** is a face of an open or closed axis the world declares receding (`receding`, section 4) with the axis's largest size and the layers per growth, both the file's numbers: beyond it the lattice grows by that many layers of zeros whenever a level other than 0 of any family stands on the layer before it, so no wave meets it (`src/event_universe/growth.py`; ALGEBRA.md, **The unbounded board**); with the axis at its largest size the run ends there, named, rather than reflecting; a face before the origin (the low side) grows by prepending, and every declared coordinate (a body's Nodes, a packet's envelope, a NodeDetector's positions, an inner face) stays the file's by the offset of the layers grown before the origin, held once per axis (`Lattice.offset`, read by `mask`; every declared Node is read on the board through `front.on_board` and returned to the file's coordinates by `front.declared`, the one offset in both directions).
- **Lattice**: the box of Nodes, `shape` [X, Y, Z] as declared, grown beyond its receding faces as the run goes (`Lattice.shape` the shape now); in code `Lattice` (`src/event_universe/lattice.py`).
- **Family**: one kind of physical value, a row of the universe file: its name, its pair [num, den], its `reads` (the holders it reads, by name, each at the integer weight it reads with and, by the held write's reciprocity, sources it with; an empty object where it reads none; required, nothing derived and nothing defaulted) and either its dimension (`dimension`, a family of quanta: any integer from 1, the lines of its record, each stepped by Rule3 as a line of dimension one, the form and the share the sum over them; 1 one real line, 2 a plane of two lines, re and im, charged matter, the one dimension with a Wronskian and a turn, 3 three real lines, neutral, which read none of the sign (`loader.universe.shape_of`); or the shape by name, a list of its lines' kinds, `real` or `plane` (1 and 2 kept as spellings; ["plane", "plane", "plane"] a record of three planes, one Wronskian per plane summed into its row of the sign, the proton's row, laid by the generator's one-Node declaration every plane alike; a record of real lines and planes together refused by name, not built); or its shape [parts, dimension], that many records of that dimension laid as one event and never summed at a Node: the pair family [2, 1], two real lines of light's pair, ALGEBRA.md #the-click-is-the-meeting) or, where it is held, `held` {`sources`: what sources it, `["form"]`, `["form", "tensions"]`, `["wronskian"]`, `["form", "tensions", "flux"]`, `["form", "tensions", "shear"]` or `["form", "tensions", "flux", "shear"]`, one real line per source (the flux and the shear three lines each); `level_weight`: the row's level weight E_s, the number of quanta of form that write one level of the row (gravity's the level weight LW of the law, ALGEBRA.md **The two rows**); `write_weight`: the row's write weight k_w, the signed integer multiplier of its one write per line (the sign holder's declared coefficient sits in the write, by decision; a nuclear holder's W_1 or -W_2), 1 in every shipped universe file but the charge's 4 in `examples/events/charged.json` and 400 in `examples/events/turning.json`, the energy line's k_w at T 36,000, every `reads` weight 1; on the massless row holding the content optionally `rest`, the vacuum content c_vac, the level at which it rests everywhere, up to every face and beyond it, 0 by decision, ALGEBRA.md #what-is-open, item 22; `act`, how the row acts on its readers, required on every held row (the file states it, no act by omission): `pace`, the plain read of its level into their paces (every holder of the content's), or `rotation`, the phase of every two-part reader's record on its Links by its level's difference across each Link, the phase line at the fixed angle 1 / Gamma per act (features/phase, section 3; the first build's turn of the record at the Node by the angle with tan(theta / 2) = its level over 2 Gamma scaled by the reader's clock, gone with V2), under which the row carries three odd axis lines beside its time line (section 3; ALGEBRA.md **The sign holder rotates the two-part record**, the engine's declared act)}. The rule derives everything else from the pair and the shape with no name ([ALGEBRA.md, every family has a dimension](ALGEBRA.md#a-familys-declaration), `src/event_universe/loader/derived.py`): a held row sourced by the form holds the content and steps at the paces of its read, the holders its declaration names among its reads and its own level among them where it names itself (ALGEBRA.md #the-paces, Every row reads the content; its lines the time line alone or with the three axis lines, the parts Rule3 reads and no other); a holder of the content carries no count, no current and no click (a declaration: the engine reads currents, shares and click lines for the families of quanta and the holder of the sign alone, `Lattice.order`, a holder's one region reading the squared deviation of its time line, section 5; the holders' travelling events as records with a count would be a design round of its own, not a fix); a held row sourced by the Wronskian holds the sign and carries quanta, its time line its record, light, alone under the plain read and with the three odd axis lines x, y, z under the rotation; every other family carries quanta (its count is its record's share and its currents the NodeDetectors' reading, readings of the record); every family reads the holders its declaration names at the weights it names (ALGEBRA.md; `derived.read_of`), a holder of the content plainly into its pace, a holder of the sign under the act `pace` as the size of its level, |L|, a hollow whatever the reader's sense (ALGEBRA.md row (f); `features.read.plain`) or, where the holder declares the rotation, as the turn of its two lines by the holder's signed time level at the Node and by the odd line of each Link it reads through, a plane's read alone (a family of real lines naming a holder under the rotation is refused by name), the holder of the sign never its own level (light reads none of the sign; a holder of the sign naming itself is refused by name), a family naming no holder reading none (the plain rule at Gamma), and each family of quanta sources what it reads at the weight it reads with, by the held write's reciprocity (the form and the tensions for a holder of the content, the Wronskian and, under the rotation, the sign's current, the mean of the Node's two a-Links' Wronskian currents, for the holder of the sign); a second level pair laid on a family whose lines are no plane is refused by name; a packet or a body is laid on every part of its family alike (the symmetric lay, `Lattice.lay`) and on every real line of a part at the line's declared weight (`weights`, the world file's key on the body or the packet, one integer per line, 1 on every line without it, `loader.keys.weights_of`: a record of three real lines laid at (a, b, c) over its lines, the pair times each weight; a weight 0 lays nothing on that line and the record stays whole for every read and every click (2026-10-21)); between clicks no family gives another anything; at a click the conversion lays a credited quantum of one family as whole quanta of other families (`conversion.converted`, `meeting.alone`; ALGEBRA.md, The conversion).
- **Exchange** (the key `exchange`): the family's declared sign under the exchange of two of its parts, `antisymmetric` or `symmetric` in the universe file, undeclared the lists and the credit as they stand; read by the lists' factor beside `full` and by the joint credit as the determinant or the permanent of the sides' parts, the antisymmetric sign read at other than two sides refused by name at load (the determinant is two sides'), nothing at the Node (act 6; ALGEBRA.md, Spin, closed for the paper as a route, The family's sign under exchange).
- **Record**: a line of the books, never a level of its own: a family's level at a Node is one number. The holder of the sign is one record of dimension 1: its time part is its real level pair, the light's record on its free row 0, the row no body owns (light its own family, the architecture of 2026-10-19 at this line's end); its odd axis lines under the rotation are the phase on the Links and no record. **The sign's rows** (ALGEBRA.md, No record reads its own write of the sign, the first of the atom's two lines): a family that reads a holder of the sign, a plane, is a charged family, and every body of it in the world file is its own record, with its own lines (`loader.derived.with_records`, the key `records` of the derived family; a packet of such a family, a packet and no standing record, adds into its first record); every holder of the sign carries one row per charged record of the world beside the free row 0, which no record owns (the laid light's row, the one row where the world has no charged body), each row the holder's lines, its time line and under the rotation its three odd lines (`records.record_slice`), the row of a record the same number in every holder of the sign (`derived.row_of`: 1 + the charged records before it in the file's order); the rows per record are the sign holder's by the law's line and no other holder's (the `wronskian` branch of `with_records`; a holder of the content, the gapped nuclear holder among them, keeps one row, which every record of its readers reads with its own write in it, the Hartree pair with the self-term; the rows per record of a content holder, designed, read cold and given up by the owner's word of 2026-10-19, ALGEBRA.md, The nuclear holder). A record's Wronskian and its sign currents are written into its own row only (`derived.row_sources`: the one record that owns the row; none for the row 0), and a record's read and its turn take the sum of every sign row but its own (`records.row_levels`), so no record reads its own write of the sign and the Hartree self-term is gone, exactly; the holder's level as the board reads it whole is the sum of the rows (`records.rows_total`), the light's record the free row 0 among them (light its own family, 2026-10-19): a NodeDetector, a body of one line and the books read that sum, and the holders of the content are sourced by the form of that sum (the cost, so the Wronskian write's balance stays exact). The second cost, stated: the rows at a Node number the world's charged records, so the bound of local information is the world's declaration (the NodeState of the holder grows by one row per charged body) and not the law's. The law's count 1 per record is the loader's gate (`loader.world.bodies_of`): a body of a charged family is one quantum of its family, one Node with the count 1, and a count above it is refused by name, the refusal naming the way to declare many quanta, one body of count 1 per quantum, each laid by the generator as the one-Node record of its quantum (the generator's one-Node declaration, the design file's `pixels`) and its own record with its own row; the four charge worlds of 10,000 quanta per record left the repository with the count-1 gate by decision (section 6), and the tests' chain body of the charged family is one quantum of count 1, laid as the one-Node record (`tests/laws.py`). Light its own family on every world, the atom world's architecture (the owner's word of 2026-10-19, the branch claude/atom-events; ALGEBRA.md, The emission of a bound record is the click's act of the taker): the family a click names, a transition's `drive` or a rate's `gives_to` (and a conversion's record out, to 2026-10-20), is a family of quanta or a holder of the sign that no family reads, whose one row is the free row 0, the light's record (the two slits' `charge` is such a holder; the Zeno worlds' `pulse` and the shelved ion's drives were, their worlds out of the tree 2026-10-20), and never the holder a charged family reads, whose rows beyond the free row are its records' own writes, refused by name (`loader/node_detector_declaration.lit_families`, the dict a body's declaration names its families from); this line re-read so: the sign holder's time part is the light's record on the free row 0, the row no body owns, and the light a charged body gives is laid as light's own record by the emission act, as the engine lays it (`emission.emitted_quantum` on the `gives_to` family, the source in time inside a guide and the packet on the open board), so no clicked record's family carries a body's static part, and the three findings of the atom's rounds are closed by it (ALGEBRA.md, the same paragraph).
- **Body**: the initial state the world file declares and no ledger: its family, its Nodes with their counts, the counts checked at the start against its record's share in quanta at those Nodes (the gate, section 3). Its Nodes at a later interval are where its family's share stands in quanta about its declared Nodes, derived when a report needs them (`reports.standing_nodes`) and never kept; its quanta that leave it stay its family.
- **NodeDetector**: the one declaration kind of every experiment (ALGEBRA.md, The NodeDetector is one declaration kind for every experiment), in two forms, each a connected region that reports and never names a Node (the one-Node NodeDetector with a record of its own on main). (1) **With Nodes alone**: a region of Nodes declared in the file to report (its `positions`, one connected region of two or more Nodes, never one Node, since it reads the net current through its boundary Ports and through one Node what enters leaves, where the NodeDetector with a record of its own above may stand on one Node, at least half the wavelength of its family's packets across the beam, q / p Nodes for the wave [p, q], the loader's gate `loader.world.regions_of_the_law`, a Node named twice refused and the extent across a periodic axis read through the wrap, the depth along the beam not gated; the Nodes of the body it names; the open faces' layer; its depth along the beam the NodeDetector's declaration), a declared NodeDetector (ALGEBRA.md #the-count-is-the-records-share, the click is the NodeDetector's report; no click names a Node), with no quantum of its own: it counts in the record's own unit W_rec = (the record's share over the board at the books' origin + count div 2) div count, read once from the credit's books and held through the run (`credit.record_unit`; the count's line Q(z) = count x W_c sin omega, so a record laid by share with its count reads W_c within the lay's rounding and one born quantum at Omega reads W_c sin Omega; the key `transition` is refused by name on every region), with its own clock, the carried sum of its Nodes' composed clocks over the board's intervals (`credit.clocked_regions`, below), optionally with its `basis`, the reader's setting (p, q), the coefficients of its credit, and its `pattern`, one integer pair [alpha_k, beta_k] per part of the record it reads, the + port reading the part k as alpha_k p + beta_k q and the - port as alpha_k (-q) + beta_k p (the pair's pattern [[1, 0], [0, 1]]: the parts read as (p, q) and (-q, p); the GHZ gate's three patterns of four parts), a declaration validated by the loader (`loader/draw.py`: a pair per part, none [0, 0], as many as the parts of the record of several parts the world lays, a pattern only with a basis) and read by the reader `tools/bell_gate.py` and by nothing in the engine; the open faces' layer is the loader's own row, named `face` and added last where an open face does not recede (`loader.faces.layer_of`), no part of the declared NodeDetector, as a body's NodeDetector is not (`NodeDetectorRow.declared`). Its **click** is its report of one interval: per family of quanta the net current into the region through the NodeDetector's front boundary Ports at its Nodes, in the current's units (`inflow`; the front: the Ports leading in from the declared board outside the declared NodeDetector, the union of the declared regions, `Lattice.declared_board`, `node_detector.node_detector_nodes`, not those between two regions nor those toward a receding face's grown layers), one `click` line per interval, family and region where it is not 0, with the region's name and never a Node; the one measurement, the host's reading for the credit by the shares (`tools/click_counts.py`); where the world declares the `draw` (section 4) its **credit** is the click written on the lattice at a window's end (`credit.py`, `features/click`): the NodeDetector draws by its read of the board, the record's remainders at its region as the digits of one number (each the remainder's index on its Node's lattice, r div F, in the base w div F + 1) modulo the weights' total, from the window's inflows (2026-10-20; CHANGED, the old kept by name: "with the declared generator"), per region for a record of one part (N whole quanta over W_c, each to a region by the regions' shares) and for a record of several parts by the sides' draws in the order of their closes with the write at the birth (J, as `tools/bell_gate.py` reads it, the gate's arithmetic; one combination of the sides' ports drawn through the root by J the engine's form until 2026-10-20), then within the credited region the one boundary Node the quantum entered through, by the window's inflows per Node, and writes there: every line of the record at that Node is set to 0 in its three arrays, the hole, exactly as the receding face removes a share (`features/click`, `act.written`: for a spread record the form is quadratic, and subtracting one quantum's levels where less than a quantum stands would add form instead of removing it); no Node writes beyond itself; the NodeDetector's front erases the clicked record inside the click's light cone, one shell per interval, the hole's act on the shell's mask, where the record's count reaches 0; the record's count goes down by one, the run's accounting in the output, and at 0 the record is uncreditable whatever its levels still show; one `credit` line per click, its result the window, the region, the family, the port realised and the parts kept, the count moved and the count left, never a Node (the NodeDetector writes at one Node and gives no result for one Node), the one Node beside it as a lattice diagnostic for the host's tool; for a family of several parts (the pair family, the GHZ family) its report carries beside the click the parts' signed level sums over its region at the interval's start, per line (one per part of a family of dimension 1) [now, before], one `parts` line per interval, family and declared region where a sum is not 0, the NodeDetector's read that the gate pairs through the root and the credit books per side (`reports.level_sums`, `reports.parts`; ALGEBRA.md #the-click-is-the-meeting); an opaque NodeDetector is a region backed by a receding face, the board's last layer before it, through which what enters leaves the declared board and never returns (a region whose back faces declared Nodes passes the density on, and its net front inflow over a run reads nothing); every other number (a level, the share, the books) is a lattice reading, a diagnostic, the `density` line among them. (2) **With a record of its own** (the former reader body) (a NodeDetector with Nodes alone is never on one Node, it is always on two Nodes or more, and a NodeDetector with a record of its own may stand on one Node, the relation seen from its two ends; ALGEBRA.md, The NodeDetector is one declaration kind for every experiment): a body of `bodies` declaring its Nodes with the lay's `weight` each (1 where absent, A_i^2 = A^2 w_i / SUM w over its connected region, one Node or more; no `count` on a reader's Node, the record's count declared once) and its `parts` (the modes' labels, one per part of its plane family of several parts, the count in one of them at the start), and as a NodeDetector its `transitions` (which arriving family's quantum moves it from which part to which, at the declared weight the arriving level is read into its phase with, and at which `resonance` [num, den], cos Omega = num / den, the pair of the transition's own frequency), its `rates` (the emission at a declared lifetime tau, one quantum of light laid at the transition's resonance: inside a guide as a source in time at the record's Node over tau intervals, in the open board as a packet of the rate's declared `width` Nodes across along a drawn axis, the loader deciding by the board's shape, `packet_form` in `loader/node_detector_declaration.py`; `src/event_universe/emission.py`; neither lays a uniform mode: the source's increments are computed whole at the emission (`emission.increments_of`) and corrected by the one lay act in time, the division act in time absorption their sum and their moment to 0 exactly (`lay.laid_in_time`; section 3, **The one lay act**), and the packet's two levels by the one lay act over the packet's Nodes (`lay.laid`, the division act in proportion to the envelope's amplitudes); the loader refuses by name a lifetime that does not hold one period of the resonance, tau Omega >= 2 pi by the rotation act (`loader/node_detector_declaration.holding_period`, `emission.holds_period` its predicate)) and its own `node_detector` (its window alone, or the empty object where the window is derived at load (CHANGED 2026-10-20, the old kept by name: "its window, its seed, its generator; the generator alone where a probe stands among its transitions"); the probe, a transition of a part into itself whose family's lay at the Node closed the body's window, left the engine 2026-10-20 at the ninth with its worlds, #2156, such a transition refused by name since), `loader/node_detector_declaration.py`, `node_detector_of`; a connected region of one Node or more by the declaration (a region in pieces refused by name, `loader.world.bodies_of`; at one Node the lay A_1 = A, the turn the drive's level at the Node exactly and the one Node drawn, `tests/test_the_node_detector.py`; its Nodes' `count` entries the lay's weights, the record's count its parts'), its lay the engine's own over its Nodes in the counts' proportion, A_i^2 = A^2 weight_i / total (no mode entry; `meeting.laid_record`, `features/click`, `spread`), its outer Links' factors 0 for its family, read 0 from both ends, and its inner Links open at G^2, so that its uniform mode rotates at cos omega_0 = num / den whatever the region's size (`meeting.cut`), held by its declaration as the NodeDetector's region is; its books the reader's own and at no Node (`node_detector.NodeBooks`); its clicks the exchange over the region, the absorption's hole at the Node drawn by the arriving record's inflow booked through its Nodes' front Ports, and the Node of the emission's lay drawn by the record's share at the body's Nodes as the board holds it at the close (`Lattice.share_of`, floored at 0), the file's lay weights entering no draw (A2 of the alignment audit; `node_detector.hole_node`, `node_detector.share_weights`, `node_detector.drawn_node`; section 3, act 6; `src/event_universe/meeting.py`, `src/event_universe/lists.py`). A body declared by its `conversion` instead of its parts, a record converted whole at its one Node drawn at the declared rate, left the engine 2026-10-20 at the ninth with its world (#2156; ALGEBRA.md, The conversion: the hypothesis stands, its code `conversion.py` and its worlds, examples/events/conversion, since 2026-10-21, the rate alone deleted). The engine's `BodyRow` and `bodies` name the file's `bodies` entries as before.
- **Packet**: a record the world file lays and no body (`packets`): a packet of a family of quanta along an axis, its wave number a fraction of pi per Link, its amplitude and its envelope declared, its levels the generator's (`tools/pixel_mode.py`, the packet lay; for a massless family the uniform mode's content taken out of each level by the one lay act's division act, `lay.corrected` through the generator's `packet_levels`, so that now and before each sum to 0 over the board and the massless row's double root at wave number 0 carries neither level nor velocity, ALGEBRA.md, The packet lay; the exact before level of a one-way packet is not compactly supported (a real packet moving one way is an analytic signal, Paley-Wiener; the laid pair's backward root stands at the analytic packet's own leakage into the mirror bins, a few 10^-4 of the form for the two slits, 3.6 x 10^-4 at the weight sin omega and 2.3 x 10^-4 at sin^2 omega), sqrt(1 - **L**^2) having the kernel of |q| / sqrt 3 near q = 0, so a packet whose quadrature sums to S level units carries a before level of order S / (2 pi sqrt 3 r^3) at r Links, of one unit at twenty Links for the two slits; the generator lays it on the board at its largest declared extent along a receding axis and crops to the board; the lattice refuses by name at its construction, through the one lay act's guard, a massless packet whose level sums are not 0; a tool that loads the world without building its board refuses nothing of this); or the same lay on a holder of the content, a kick: the row's own travelling events added to its rest after the start, no count and no source (`tests/test_the_node.py`, the vacuum content's test).
- **Interval**: one step of the whole lattice; `intervals` intervals make a run.

## 2. The NodeState

The engine is twenty-seven modules: `flux.py` (the step, Rule3 in flux form: every line of every record stepped by the law's line, the Links' sum gathered Link by Link, `flux.link_fluxes`, a turned or folded plane by the one complex line with the Link's pair, `flux.turned_link_fluxes`, the click's face as the value in the place of one arrival, `flux.face_differences`, the division `core/rule3.rule3`; section 3), `node.py` (the Node's reads: the content and the Links' factors, the rule's integers of that read, `node.rule_of`, the sign's levels a turned record reads, the phase act on the holder's phase lines, `node.phased`, and the one write per held part), `plane.py` (the Link's pairs of a plane under the rotation in the fold form: each Link's read as a pair (R^c_ij, R^s_ij) = p_i^2 (X^c_ij, X^s_ij), gravity's pair from the flux holder's odd lines, `plane.fold_pairs`, the sign's from the sign holder's odd lines by the tangent half-angle triple at the wall 4 Gamma, `plane.sign_pairs`, folded with the Link's phase pair, `plane.phase_links`, `plane.link_pairs`; the step reads the pair as (R^c_ij + i R^s_ij) z_j in Rule3's two numerators over the wall unchanged; no turn of the level before and none after), `covector.py` (the Lorentz form on the sign holder's four lines, the universe file's key `form`: `lorentz`: the time line and the three odd lines one covector at the weights (-3, 1, 1, 1), `covector.weights`, the odd line of the axis a the +a Link's own, sourced by the Link's own flux of the record's step and read by that Link alone, `covector.link_fluxes`, and the lattice Lorenz constraint's gate at the lay, `covector.gate`), `records.py` (the NodeState and the lines: a line of dimension one, the lines of one record, light as the sum of the sign's rows, the sum of every row but the reader's own, the form, the Wronskian, the well as a reading, the write's factor and the write's numerators row by row), `bookings.py` (the bookings per record, light's form from its rows' sum, and the sources of the start row by row, the one act the start and the generator share), `share.py` (the share, the count as a reading of the record; under the rotation read on the level pair as the step reads it, `share.share`, the currents from the same pair), `reports.py` (the NodeDetectors' reports, a body's Nodes derived for a report), `credit.py` (the credit's books of a window: the inflows booked through the front per region and boundary Node, the sides' second moments (`Books.moments`, nothing joint since 2026-10-20), the counts and the units, the window counted, the number left of a close's read per record and side), `digits.py` (the click's read of the board, section 3: Rule3's kept remainders of the records a detector reads at its Nodes as the digits of one number, each the remainder's index on its Node's lattice F = gcd(w, D_i, R_ij) in the base w div F + 1, on a turned plane gcd(w, D_i, R_ij, R^c_ij, R^s_ij) with the Link pairs the step reads, `flux.pairs_of` at the record's read, p_i^2 at the vacuum paces (2026-10-21, the reader's ruling on issue #2169; the plain reaches alone gave the hydrogen electron w div 9), `digits.digits_of`, `reads_of`, `at_origin`, `lattice_of`, `read_of`, the order `loader/draw.DIGIT_ORDER`; the read refused by name at the lay's origin and on the exact vacuum's three-valued lattice), `meeting.py` (the meeting at a Node, the NodeDetectors' loop: the records at Nodes read every interval, the window's turn of the labels, the clicks drawn at the close through the one act, the emission's at its grain, the absorptions', the null window's and the region NodeDetectors' at the credit's window, the lay of a packet whole at its declared interval, the Links cut at 0, and the region NodeDetectors' lists assembled across the families at the credit's window's close, the families credited together met at one Node drawn by the product of their inflows per Node with their one irreducible list and each family's quanta alone with their conversions, `meeting.regions`, `meeting.met`, `meeting.alone`, 2026-10-21; the probe's and the conversion's clicks to 2026-10-20), `meeting_back.py` (the one meeting, section 3, act 6: on a record of several parts read at declared sides each side draws at its own place on its local form, `side_weights` with `gram_of` and `coefficients_of`, the record runs back to its birth by the inverse with the faces and the lays re-presented from the books' log, `run_back`, `birth_of`, `logged`, `crossed`, the realised port is written there, `realised`, and the record runs forward again by the step, `run_forward`, N runs for N sides; the back-in-time gate's crossing and comparison, `faces_from`, `snapshot`, `first_difference`, moved into it from the tool, which imports them), `cone.py` (the cone stepped alone, 2026-10-20, section 3, act 6: in the cone's reach the run back and the run forward step the record's cone and nothing else, the view of the ball's box with its ring over every family's arrays, `box_of` and `viewed`, stepped by the engine's own step with no act and `step_inverse`; the ring and the box's Nodes outside the record's cone read from the board's own arrays and asserted equal to the lay's frame 0 regrown, `held`, never substituted; the computed Nodes outside the cone of the interval arrived at asserted at frame 0 after the step, the box's edge discarded, `edge_of`; the acts' logs clipped to the box with the refusal of an act outside the cone, `acts_inside`; the ball's Nodes written back into the board, `written_back`; the growths of the receding faces popped on the way back and replayed from the log on the way forward; the Node-steps counted per interval into the books, `counted`; `stepped_alone` the one call from `meeting_back`), `act.py` (the one act of the click: the draw among a click's alternatives, the Node's act for every Item of the drawn list, the face and the lay, and the click's line after it, `act.click`, `act.written`), `lists.py` (the lists of the click from the declaration, one table: the region's credit of one family before any draw, `meeting.credited`, and the credited Node with its line, `lists.credited_node`, the sides' sequence, the absorption, the emission and the null window, each row the alternatives of one draw; the probe's close gone 2026-10-20), `take.py` (the take, the act's readings and items, the law's word for the act its contents serve, so named 2026-10-21 and `twist.py` until then, 190 lines since 2026-10-20: the taker's part re-laid at each Node's own direction, `take.relaid`, `take.levels_at`, the click's items, `take.Item`, the hole's faces, `take.faced`, `take.faces_presented`, `take.faces_reported`, the piece's momentum readings, `take.fan_momentum`, `take.scaled`, the piece's source drawn among the emitters by the quanta each holds and named with the recoil it owes, -p_a as integers on the credit line and written nowhere, `take.recoiled`, `take.drawn_emitter`, the loss booked by name, `take.unpaid`; the fold's `twisted`, `folded_lines`, `folded_source` and `Probe` deleted 2026-10-20 on the branch engine-clean, `quarter_turn` moved to `features/phase` as a reading), `readings.py` (the readings of a record's lines at every Node, one Link's reach each: the six arrivals in Port order, the currents through the Ports, the tension's part and the axis sources, the sign's current and the lattice momentum per axis), `board_readings.py` (the board's readings, each one loop over the lattice's records and no act: every family of quanta's currents through the Ports, every record's tension's part per axis with the count's flux and, where a held row declares the shear, the shear's part per axis pair, every turned record's sign current or, under its holder's Lorentz form, its step's own flux through the Node's +a Links, and a held family's uniform mode, the time line's mean level, a lattice diagnostic), `resonance.py` (the resonant read of a record declared a NodeDetector: the two reference records at the declared pair, the scale, the window's two sums and its turn, the labels' hop by the phase line's pair with the carried division), `conversion.py` (the conversion, a click's act outside the step, derived for every family of quanta with a gap and a Pythagorean pair and declared by no key (the key `converts` and its table deleted 2026-10-21 by the owner's word): the one list of whole quanta exact iff every pair is Pythagorean and the Gaussian product of the records out equals the parent's point, keeping the charge's Wronskian sum and the baryon's count, a family with no list never converting, several lists refused by name at load, `conversion.exact`, `conversion.declared`; the senses of a real-line parent's plane products the row key `senses`, the one residual declaration; the when the region's window's close of the arriving record's passage; the act the credit's hole and one lay by the count per record out at the credited Node with the planes' exact split, `conversion.converted`, `meeting.alone`, `meeting.met`, `emission.laid_by_count`, `features/click.plane_squares`; several parents credited at one close the same rule with their rotations' product and the irreducible list alone, `conversion.reducible`, `conversion.rows_at`, 2026-10-21), `emission.py` (the emission as a source in time: one quantum laid at the record's Node over the lifetime at the transition's declared resonance, the total the form the source radiates, `emission.radiated_total`, the lay by the count on every laid line alike with the sense, `emission.laid_by_count`, `emission.emitted_lines`, the open board's packet along a drawn axis, `emission.laid_packet`, and the born unit, `emission.born_unit`), `node_detector.py` (the NodeDetector's region: its books over its Nodes, the lay's amplitudes in the weights' proportion and the norm, the arriving level projected on the normalised mode, the inflow booked per Node over the window, the dark and the clock over the region, the outcome's draw and the drawn Node, the click line that names no Node; no level written there), `front.py` (the erasing front: the hole's act on the shell at the Link-metric distance of the intervals since a record's count reached 0, a loop's act beside the receding face; and the file-to-board offset, `front.on_board` and `front.declared`), `lay.py` (the one lay act: every write of a record's levels from outside Rule3 as one call of `lay.laid` over Nodes or `lay.laid_in_time` over a span, the division act that takes a massless record's uniform part out and the guard on the two sums, section 3), `growth.py` (the receding face: the lattice grown by layers of zeros where the front reaches it, the layers taken off on the way back), `lattice.py` (the lattice that walks the interval forward and back) and `world_files.py` with `loader/` (the files; `loader/universe.py` the universe file, `loader/node_detector_rows.py` a reader's count declared once by its parts or its `count`, `loader/draw.py` the draw's declaration and a region's setting with its pattern, `loader/node_detector_declaration.py` a body's declaration as a NodeDetector with a record of its own, `loader/derived.py` the families from the rule, each held family's one write per part with its walls, `derived.held_write_of`, and the amplitude bound), on `core/` (Rule3, the Ports, the composed paces `paces.py`, which holds the clock and the Node's pace as the law's two functions of the content with the write's factor and the turn's, and the working bound; `rule3.largest_below`, the largest x with x^2 <= a count by a bisection on the share reading, the one search in place of every root) and the folders of `features/` (`click`, `currents`, `front`, `held_write`, `phase`, `read`, `start`, `write`).

`src/event_universe/records.py`, `NodeState`, one per family, every array over the lattice of the kind the file's width chooses at load (`World.kind`, section 4; the hardware's 64-bit integers at a width at or under the host's, Python's integers above it):

| Field | Held by | What it is |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `lines` (each `now`, `before`, `remainder`) | every family alike | its lines, each a record of dimension one (two levels and Rule3's remainder), as many as the loader derives for the family (`FamilyRule.lines`): a family of quanta's record, its dimension's lines per part, one real line, a plane's two (re then im) or the three real lines of a record of dimension 3, the pair family's two parts two real lines; a held row's sources' count, the time line first and then, where the tensions source it, the three axis lines xx, yy, zz, then the flux's three odd lines where it carries them and last the three shear lines xy, yz, zx where its declaration names the shear (2026-10-21); the holder of the sign's time line is its record, light, and under the act `rotation` the three odd axis lines x, y, z follow it, each the phase on that axis's Links, and it carries these lines once per row, the free row 0 and one row per charged record of the world (`FamilyRule.records`; light the rows' sum, `records.rows_total`); a charged family's lines are its records' in the world's order, each record a body; a row with a gap as a row without one |
| `write_remainders` | a held family | one remainder per line of its one write, in [0, wall), at half the wall from the start: the time line's wall E_s T, each tension line's E_s W_c and each odd line's E_s T, the time line's own (`derived.held_write_of`); empty for a family that holds nothing |
| `phases` (each line `now`, `before`, `remainder`) | the holder of the sign under the rotation | per row of the sign beyond the free row, six lines of the kind "phase": the cosine and the sine line of each axis's Link at the Node owning it (the +a Link of i), Rule3's rotation act at the fixed angle theta_0 = 1 / Gamma (D = 2 Gamma^2, K_0 = D - 1), seeded at the amplitude X the width derives ((X, X cos theta_0, D div 2) and (0, -(X div Gamma), D div 2), `features/phase.seed`, `phase.amplitude`), iterated |n_ij| acts per interval in the sense of n_ij = L(i) - L(j), the row's reader's potential difference across the Link (`node.phased`), and read by the Port as the hop's pair (X cos theta, X sin theta) inside the sign's fold (`plane.phase_links`, `plane.link_pairs`); 18 integers per Node per record; empty for the free row and for every other family (the temporal gauge: the time Link carries no phase, the time turn's shears gone) |

The engine's step receives lines with their coefficients, their sources and their readers and knows no family, no dimension and no name; the lattice groups the lines into families as the loader derived them, for the readings and the sources alone. No count, no sense and no remainder of either stands at a Node: a family of quanta's count is its record's share, e_i = [w (now^2 + before^2) - S now before - now SUM over the six Ports of R_ij before_j] div (2 p_i^2 G^2) at the paces of the read, the conserved form's term at the Node with the weight 1 / p_i^2 of the Node's pace over the vacuum's 2 G^2 (the plain share 3 den (now^2 + before^2) - num now S_6(before) in the vacuum, ALGEBRA.md #the-conserved-form), in quanta (e_i + W_c div 2) div W_c over the wall W_c = 3 den T (`share.share`, `share.family_share`, `share.quanta_of`, `Lattice.share_of`, `Lattice.quanta`); its currents are F_ij = num (now_i before_j - before_i now_j) through each Port, every line's added (`readings.currents_of`, `features/currents`); its form D_i = now^2 - next x before summed over its lines and its Wronskian W_i, the bilinear of a plane's two lines (the integer 0 for real lines, the pair family's among them), are read about the step (`records.form`, `records.wronskian`), the writes' sources, its sign current, the mean of its two a-Links' Wronskian currents, J_a / 2 with J_a = Im(conj(z_i) (z_(+a) - z_(-a))) = re_i (im_(+a) - im_(-a)) - im_i (re_(+a) - re_(-a)) = (G_(i, i-a) - G_(i, i+a)) / num, the net of the conserved current through the Node's two a-Ports from the levels now, the mean (J_a + 1) div 2 rounded as the read rounds the Link's tension, odd under the sense within that unit (`readings.sense_current_of`), the odd lines' source under the rotation, and the well D_i div T is a reading (`records.well`); every one of them read from the record and kept nowhere (ALGEBRA.md #the-count-is-the-records-share; #the-primitives, a family's write is one act). The structural gate `tests/test_rule3.py::test_the_generic_node_is_closed_for_building` holds this table to the code's fields.

## 3. The interval

Before the first interval every held family's time part stands at its sources' rest (`Lattice.start`; ALGEBRA.md #what-a-body-is, the four lines (a) and (c), the body stands where the sources return themselves and the well D_i div T is the record's quanta as the fields' source): the sources the form of the laid records as the held write's write books it, D_i = now^2 - next x before over the record's lines with next the step of Rule3 at the paces of the held rows' rests, for a held row sourced by the form, and the Wronskian of the step's booking for the holder of the sign (its rest, sources of either sign; every row of it rests under the Wronskian of the one record that owns it, the row 0 under nothing, `derived.row_sources`), each at the weight with which the record's family reads the row (the held write's reciprocity), and every held row's record at the start its laid packet plus its sourced rest, the level now and the level before alike (the rest the static solution of the row's line at the paces and the laid packet a travelling one, the line linear at fixed paces, so their sum is the record; the laid levels 0 where nothing is laid, the record then the rest, and a holder of the sign nothing sources stands as laid), over the write's wall E_s T, scaled per proper volume and per proper interval at the paces of the rest as the write scales it; the lay and the rest iterated to the fixed point (`features/start`, `held_rests`): from nothing, the rests laid, every record stepped once at their paces and booked as the held write books it (`Lattice.stepped`, `records.form`, `records.wronskian`), the rests solved again from the bookings, until the levels repeat the state they were booked at, or repeat an earlier state one unit off at most at every Node, a rounding tie, a return further off refused by name as a cycle (`returned`, the one rule of every repeat of the start), so that the held write's first write returns the start's rest within Rule3's rounding, two units at most at any Node (`tests/test_the_bound_body.py`; the share in quanta of the laid record is the gate's reading and no source: a body laid by the generator as the standing record of a well sourced by its share stands in a shallower well here, the binding holder's level at the pixel's centre 725 against 870, and its declared count, the generator's reading at the deeper paces, is admitted only within the gate's rounding); within each booking every holder of the content at the rest of its own line at the paces its reads give it, the holders its declaration names among the content at their weights, its own level among them where it names itself (`settled_rows`, `read_content`: the holders' rests taken in turn until every row's levels repeat, or the rows together repeat an earlier state one unit off, a rounding tie, under the same rule; each row's rest the division act iterated from nothing until the levels repeat, the first state the act returns unchanged, and then refined to the line within one fine unit at every Node by the scaled residual (`features/start`, `settled`, `refined`: the floored iteration stops below the line by up to one fine unit over 1 - rho in the board's lowest mode, rho the row's Jacobi factor, 137 fine units on the open 25-cube at the massless pair, 9 levels at the row's unit 15 there; at the stop the line's residual is read exactly in integers, in [0, W), the same line is solved for the correction with the residual scaled by a power of two F as its source by the same act to its own stop, and the correction is added rounded half up, F the least at or above twice the miss's bound K that the width admits for the round, else the largest it admits with the rounds repeated while F is below 2 K, K the least of two bounds where both hold (`bound_of`, the smaller where both hold): the parabola's (6 div 2) ((n + 2) div 2)^2 + 1 along the longest open or closed axis of n Nodes, the six reads over the axis's two Ports times the half-extent squared, plus one (`PORTS`, `SIDES`; the inverse of 6 b - S_6(b) = 6 along the axis is the parabola 3 x (n + 1 - x), which the massless line at the vacuum's paces returns at or above the unit source), and, where the row has a gap W - 6 R > 0 at every Node, W div (W - 6 R) + 1 at the Node where it is largest (den div (den - num) + 1 at the vacuum's paces); the gap bound alone where every axis of more than one Node wraps, the parabola alone where an inner face is the sink, and a refusal by name where no face bounds it; every number the width's and the board's; the fine unit itself is derived from a bound on the rest's levels (`unit_of`; the unit the largest at which the six reads at the vacuum's paces on a rest within the bound, taken twice for the two levels the line sums, stay inside the width, `unit_from_bound`, written from `coefficients` and no number): the tent of the source spread flat over the longest extent where it leaves a unit, as every shipped world has it (the tent, `tent_of`: half of 3 x the source total over the wall x (the extent + 1), plus one; on a chain Rule3's line at the massless pair, the folded axes' four reads falling on the Node itself, is 2 b_x - b_(x-1) - b_(x+1) = 3 sigma, so a source spread flat over n Nodes raises the discrete parabola b_x = 3 sigma x (n + 1 - x) / 2, its top 3 S (n + 1) / 8 at S = n sigma, under the tent by four, and a point source at the centre 3 S (n + 1) / 4, under it by two; the 3 Rule3's source coefficient and the 2 the parabola's half, no number of the engine), and where it leaves none, on a board open on every axis of more than one Node (a periodic axis folds the source's images onto it and the rest grows past the bound), the source total over its wall plus one, since a point source's rest on the cubic lattice peaks at 3 G(0) of its source over the wall, G(0) = 0.2527 below one third, so the tent over-bounds it by 2 (L + 1) and held the atom's Node clock at a fiftieth of its reach, the refusal by name where both leave none; the finding and the remedy, closed in the fix round: on a point source of 3,000 quanta at the centre of the open 25-cube at the divisor 1 the rests of the massless row and the binding row stand within 0.51 level of the line's exact solve at every Node, 1682 and 1677 at the centre against 1682.19 and 1676.55, where the stop alone left them 1677 and 1674, `tests/test_the_features.py`), (6 (den - num) p_0^2 + 6 num p_i^2) b = num p_i^2 S_6(b) + 3 den Gamma^2 sigma at the composed clock and the Node's pace of the row's own level as last rounded, sigma the row's write weight times the source scaled per proper volume and per proper interval at those paces as the write scales it (`features/start`, `scaled_source`, `paces.write_factor`; `booked_sources`, `FamilyRule.write`: two proper-interval powers on a count, one on a Wronskian, the three axes' paces the Node's since no tension stands at the start), the paces re-read from the rounded levels until they repeat the content they were read at, every row's rest in a pass of the start seeded with its rest of the pass before (`settled_rows`, `held_rests`) and each re-read's correction with the re-read before's, brought to the round's scale (`refined`, `seed`), the same fixed points reached from nearer (the massless rest of the point source on the 25-cube 8,812 passes against 11,676 unseeded and 1,312 at the stop alone) (the fixed point, or an earlier state one unit off at most at every Node, a rounding tie; re-read levels that return to an earlier state further off, a cycle of two states or more, are refused by name with the pair, the cycle's length and the two levels at the Node, no rest laid), 6 den b = num S_6(b) + 3 den sigma where the content is 0, at a fine unit from the host's width (the file's where the host's leaves none, so a file wider than the host lays the same levels), the sources of either sign or both where the board has a sink or the pair a gap, the source over the row's level weight, refused by name where the content reaches the Link's zero at a Node (`paces.frozen_content`), the row's own pace rounded to 0, a frozen clock, `RestCollapses`, which no body within the width's reach meets under the composed paces, the 50-quanta chain body resting at the content 959, U = 0.16), then the holder of the sign at the rest of its line under the content it reads, none of its own level among it, with or without a gap; the same iteration serves the generator, which calls the engine's own start on each record it lays (`tools/pixel_mode.py`, `start_content`; the booking `booked_sources`, one function for both, taken for every held holder under the rotation after the first interval's phase act with the phase lines returned to the angle 0 exactly, 2026-10-21, so that the Wronskian booked is the first step's and the first write returns the rest; the shipped booking at the seed angle 0 kicked every turned world's row at the first write, 567 levels at the 11-cube atom's centre, 3029 to 3596, closed by name the same day), the count's rest its seed alone, the remainder at the half wall of the rule the row steps by, a row with no source at 0 with the same remainder; every write remainder stands at half its wall from the state's making (`records.empty_state`, `records.write_origins`), the walls derived from the rows (`derived.held_write_of`: E_s T for the time part, E_s x 3 den T for each tension part with the sourcing families' den, their least common multiple where they differ, and E_s T, the time part's own wall, for each odd axis line of a holder under the rotation); a row with a `rest` has it added at every Node, both levels, its remainder at the half wall with or without a source, and reads it through every Port beyond an open or inner face (`flux.step_line`, `fill`), the same vacuum on both sides, so a uniform rest is the row's own fixed point bit for bit; then every kick is added to its row; the axis parts start at 0. Then the guard, once: every family of quanta's read of the initial state is checked as squares, the content below the Link's zero at every Node (the lower side: a pace is never below 0 under the composed paces, the branch below 0 met by no content, and at `paces.frozen_content`, 28,206 at Gamma 6,000, the Node's pace rounds to 0, a frozen clock, refused by name) and p^2 (den + |num|) <= 2 den Gamma^2 on the clock's square, the Node's pace squared and each Link's pace squared, p_i^2 Q_ij over G^2 (the upper side, a hill's; the band's lowest mode at or above -2, at wave number pi for a numerator at or above 0 and at wave number 0 for one below it, the audit's witness [-1, 2] at Gamma 4 with the content -1 refused where den + num admitted it; the guard excludes exponential growth and admits the edge itself, a repeated root the line grows linearly, light's vacuum checkerboard among them), with every Link's factor above 0 (`node.guarded`, `features/read`; a family that reads nothing stands at Gamma), refused by name outside, and under the rotation each Link's two ends' sum of the sign's odd lines within 4 Gamma, |L_a(i) + L_a(i + a)| <= 4 Gamma (read with the hop's sign, -(L_a(i) + L_a(i + a)), since 2026-10-21, `FamilyRule.link_read` (-1, -1)), the tangent half-angle at most 1 (`plane.link_guard`), and on a folded record the fold's tangent at most 1 on every Link, 6 K |V_ij| <= Gamma^2 (`plane.fold_guard`), refused by name naming the Port; the time level guarded by nothing, the phase line's circle wrapping (features/phase); no act of the interval reads a pace or an angle to refuse. Then the gate (`Lattice.gate`): every body's declared count c against its family's share in quanta read at its declared Nodes at the paces of the read, ((|c - read| - 1) div 2)^2 <= c, refused by name beyond it, a reading of the laid record and no lay; the books' origin is every family's share as laid (`Lattice.laid`). Every line of every family is born with its remainder at the half wall of the rule it steps by, w div 2, the vacuum (0, 0, w div 2) at every Node, the lay's origin and the start's alike, the layers grown beyond a receding face the same (`records.empty_record`, `Lattice.half_wall`, `growth.resized`), so that the one rounding of Rule3 is half up at every Node and no neighbour reads a floor; under the floor (the remainder born at 0) a lone massless quantum laid at one Node of an even periodic box grew as t^2 on the uniform mode's double root, 6,268 levels at the interval 200 against the rule's 172); every shipped world's levels move within the roundings' floor and are read again by name in a readings round, not bit for bit. The order at the start (2026-10-19): the credit's books are made before the gate on the declared counts (`credit.Books.of`, then `Lattice.gate`), so a family whose packets carry two units is refused by name before any count is read (C1, `credit.record_unit`).

The back-in-time gate, `tools/back_in_time.py` (`PYTHONPATH=src python tools/back_in_time.py --intervals 40 <world>.json`), runs a world N intervals forward after its first interval (fewer where the run ends at a receding face's largest size, the end named in the verdict) and as many back and compares every array of every family (every line's two levels and its remainder, every held line's write remainder), at the shape of that interval, and the interval counter with the state at the same interval on the way forward: MATCH, or MISS naming the first interval, the array (a shape that differs among them) and the first Node that differ; `tests/test_the_node.py` runs it on the closed cube over twelve intervals, on the hand chain over 24 with a rotating body of dimension 2 and with a real body alike (the gate is bit for bit per step, so the short run asserts the rule), and on the chain of light alone with both faces receding over its run to the largest size, `tests/test_pixel_mode.py` on the slit world over its run; on the three gate worlds, `examples/events/two_slits/two_slits.json` over 130 intervals, Bell's four chains of `examples/events/bell/` over their 100 and the GHZ's four over their 60, it says MATCH across the faces, the clicks of the region NodeDetectors, every erasure interval and the lays (the absorption, the emission, the null window), which the tool undoes from the `lay` lines before each step back (act 6, the face form): on the fix round's engine (the write's one rounding and the refined rest) all nine say MATCH and every world's number is unmoved, N 278, S 478 / 169, M -4; the recipe's `--intervals 40` on the two slits' world prints `{"verdict": "MATCH", "intervals": 40, "interval": 1, "ended": null}`, the tool's own report (a run on main 637a062; section 6, step 5). The readings: `books()` per family of quanta (`share`, its share summed over the lattice in the current's units; `quanta`, the same over W_c; `drift`, the share against the one the world started with, Rule3's own rounding and the paces' own change over the run; `pace`, the least Node pace p_i = p_0^2 / Gamma of the final state, a diagnostic; `frozen`, the count of its frozen Nodes, with `share`, `quanta` and `drift` None while there is one), `Lattice.quanta` and `Lattice.share_of` per family of quanta (its share at every Node with the mask of its frozen Nodes, the Node's pace rounded to 0 at a content at or beyond the Link's zero, `paces.frozen_content`, 28,206 at Gamma 6,000 and beyond the width's reach of any body, where the share is not read and no number is invented, `share.frozen`; ALGEBRA.md #the-count-is-the-records-share, the frozen Node) and `Lattice.body_nodes` per body (where its family's share stands about its declared Nodes, a frozen Node among them standing).

**The one lay act** (`src/event_universe/lay.py`; ALGEBRA.md, No write from outside Rule3 wakes either double root of the massless row): every write of a record's levels from outside Rule3 is one call of `lay.laid`, the act's change of the level now and of the level before at every Node with the lay's weights per Node, or of `lay.laid_in_time`, the source in time's increments over its span. For a massless family the division act takes the uniform part out of each level's change in proportion to the weights, the leftover one unit each at the heaviest Nodes in x-major order (`lay.division_act`, the packet lay's own act; in time `lay.division_act_in_time`, the velocity's sum among the increments in proportion to the amplitudes and the moment among the running sums in proportion to the parabola); a gapped family takes no correction. The guard (`lay.guarded`) refuses by name an act whose change of SUM now or of SUM before over the board (in time, of the sum or of the first moment over the span) is not 0, reading the act's change and never the sums themselves, which Rule3's own floors walk. The write step (`lay.written`, the one place a level is written from outside Rule3) adds the changes at the lay's Nodes, sets the remainder of every written Node to the origin the door names (the half wall of the family's rule for a fresh lay, as it stands for a change of a standing record, each door naming its choice at the call) and reports one `lay` line per written Node whose levels or remainder moved. The doors: the generator's packet lay (`tools/pixel_mode.py`, `packet_levels`, through `lay.corrected`) and the loader's lay of the mode file's levels (`Lattice.lay`, the weights all 0, no correction and the guard alone, the lattice's refusal at its construction of a massless packet whose two sums are not 0, the loader building no board and refusing nothing of this; the lays of the start report no line, the lattice receiving its output after its construction); the source in time's increments (`emission.emitted_quantum`, `emission.laid_increment`); the open board's packet (`emission.laid_packet`, the weights the envelope's amplitudes); the lay by the count (`emission.laid_by_count`, the whole packet, and the conversion's records out to 2026-10-20, a one-Node lay whose uniform part is the lay itself, laid as built and named); and the absorption's lay of a record declared a NodeDetector (`take.relaid`, laid as built); an emission into a beam, the receiving record's booked share at the Node above its quantum, opens no door (`act.written`, the undepleted beam, section 3, act 6). The loader refuses by name an emission whose lifetime does not hold one period of its resonance, num / den at most cos(2 pi / tau) by the rotation act, naming the lifetime and the least admitted span (`loader/node_detector_declaration.holding_period`, `emission.holds_period` its predicate); the shelved ion's fluorescence gives over 8 intervals. The erasing front's writes are Rule3's own at the faces it presents (`src/event_universe/front.py`) and no lay. The lay's test room is `tests/test_the_lay.py`: the division act and the guard, the write step's origin and lines, the span's refusal, and every door's act on a massless record leaving the two sums as it found them with the back-in-time gate reading MATCH across it. The lay act lays what the mode file holds and derives nothing (2026-10-19, the owner's word): the standing lay of a charged record in a holder's well is the generator's, tools/standing_lay.py, Rule3's own standing solution from the holder's rows the start writes, generic for any plane record reading a holder of the sign and any level named by its kind, with no profile set by hand; the sign under exchange is the universe file's declaration on a family of several parts, read by the lists and the credit alone (act 6, the factor beside `full`; the joint form's determinant or permanent, `credit.terms_of`), nothing at the Node.

**Rule3 in flux form, the law's symbols against the code's identifiers** (ALGEBRA.md #the-line, the flux form; the owner's word of 2026-10-16: the engine matches the law in its implementation): the line w (a_next - 2 a_now + a_before) + r' - r = SUM over the Node's six Links of R_ij (m_ij - a_now) - D_i a_now is written once, in `core/rule3.rule3`, and the step (`flux.py`) hands it the Links' sum gathered Link by Link; one row per symbol. The cost per interval, the median of a run on one host against 2.0.0's step: the time paper's run (400x8x8) 45.8 -> 51.3 ms, +12 percent; pixel (25x25x25) 81.5 -> 69.2 ms, -15 percent; the resident memory unchanged.

| the law's symbol | what it is | the identifier | where |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| a_now | the Node's level the step starts from (the level now forward, the level before backward) | `now` (`level` in the step) | `rule3.rule3`, `flux.step_line` |
| a_before | the Node's other level, left behind (forward) or returned to (backward) | `other` | `rule3.rule3`, `flux.step_line` |
| a_next | the level the step writes | the quotient `rule3` returns, `found` | `rule3.carried_division`, `flux.step_line` |
| r, r' | the remainder before and after, in [0, w) | `remainder`, the second return of `rule3` | `rule3.rule3`, `rule3.carried_division` |
| w | the family's wall, 6 den Gamma^2 G^2 | `wall` | `rule3.wall_of`, `Rule.wall` of `node.rule_of` |
| R_ij | the reach of the Link to the neighbour j, read from the Node's end, 2 num p_i^2 Q_ij G^2 | `reaches` (one per Port in Port order; `reads` where the first form named them) | `rule3.rule_integers`, `Rule.reaches` of `node.rule_of` |
| D_i | the Node's mass, 12 (den - num) p_0^2 G^2, 0 for light | `mass` | `rule3.mass`, `Rule.mass` of `node.rule_of` |
| j | the neighbour at the far end of one of the Node's six Links, the Links in Port order +X, -X, +Y, -Y, +Z, -Z | `port`, the index of the Port into `reaches`, `pairs` and the six arrivals | `core/ports.PORT_SIDES`, `readings.ports`, `flux.link_fluxes` |
| S_i | the own weight of the law's first form, 2 w - D_i - SUM_j R_ij, read by the form's Node term (`rule3.form_term`, `share.py`) and by the loader's rooms; not read by the step | `self_coefficient` | `rule3.rule_integers`, `Rule.self_coefficient` of `node.rule_of` |
| m_ij | the record the Link sends: the neighbour's level, the fill beyond a face or at a Node beyond the board (the rest on a held row's time line, 0 elsewhere), the Node's own level on an axis of one layer, the neighbour's pair turned by the Link's pair on a turned Link, the click's value at a faced Node | `level[far]` / `level[near]`, `fill`, the pair product in `turned_link_fluxes`, `face_differences` | `flux.link_fluxes`, `flux.turned_link_fluxes`, `flux.face_differences` |
| SUM_j R_ij (m_ij - a_now) | the Links' sum, the flux | `flux` | `flux.link_fluxes`, `flux.gathered_line`, `flux.gathered_plane`, the first argument of `rule3.rule3` |
| (R^c_ij, R^s_ij) | a turned Link's pair, p_i^2 (X^c_ij, X^s_ij) | `pairs` (`evens`, `odds` per Port) | `plane.link_pairs`, `flux.pairs_of` |
| sigma | the direction, +1 forward, -1 back | `direction` | `rule3.rule3`, `rule3.carried_division` |
| the order of the sum | w (2 a_now - a_before), then - D_i a_now, then + the Links' sum, then + r, the order the amplitude bound is derived for | `wall * (now + now - other) - mass * now + flux` | `rule3.rule3` |
| A | the amplitude bound, the engine's and no line of the law | `World.amplitude_bound`, `loader/derived.amplitude_bound` by `bound_under_rooms` over `rule3.partial_rooms` | `loader/derived.bound_under_rooms`, section 4 |

The law's first form, SUM over the Ports of R_ij m_ij + S_i a_now - w a_before + r, the same integers by the identity S_i = 2 w - D_i - SUM_j R_ij, is written in no file of `src/`; `tools/step_oracle.py` keeps it as `arrivals_rule3`, the step's oracle, and runs the engine against it on a world (`tests/test_step_oracle.py` runs two worlds in the suite); every example world and the time paper's run were run with it, forward and back with the record to the lay.

## 4. The input

A run reads three files, all JSON: the world file, the universe file it names by its repository path, and the generator's mode file beside it (the engine start file, `{}` since the audit and `{"mode": "check"}` before it, left with the key `engine` on 2026-10-20, the ninth commit), `<world>.mode.json`. `src/event_universe/world_files.py` (`load_world`) reads them and `src/event_universe/loader/world.py` (`parse_world`, with `loader/universe.py` for the universe file, `loader/keys.py`, `loader/faces.py`, `loader/packets.py`, `loader/mode.py`, `loader/draw.py`, `loader/node_detector_declaration.py` and `loader/lay.py`) checks every key and refuses every other key as unknown by name; no default is written, and a refusal names the key, the value and the reason (`REFUSED` in the output, section 5).

| File | Keys |
| --- | --- |
| world | `shape` [X, Y, Z] (an axis of extent 1 folds, with the wrap on it); `boundary` {`x`, `y`, `z`: `open`, `periodic` or `closed`} (a massless family on a board periodic on all three axes with every extent even is refused by name, the staggered mode (-1)^(x + y + z + t), the band's top, being an exact mode of such a board walked by Rule3's own rounding without bound, `loader/world.even_box_refused`; one odd extent or an open axis admits it); `face_depth` (from 1, required with an open axis); optionally `faces`, the inner faces, each `axis` (`x`, `y` or `z`), `at` (its coordinate on that axis) and `gaps`, a list of gaps each a range [first, last] under the name of each of the other two axes (the plane's Nodes outside every gap are beyond the board; faces that leave no Node are refused); optionally `receding`, the receding faces, per axis (`x`, `y` or `z`, open or closed and of two Nodes or more) an object with `sides` (a list of `low` and/or `high`, each once), `largest` (the axis's largest extent in Nodes, above the shape's) and `layers` (the layers of zeros per growth, from 1), every key required and every other word refused by name; `intervals`; `universe` (a repository path; `engine`, the engine start file's path, left with the ninth commit, 2026-10-20); `bodies`, a list of bodies, each `family` (a family of quanta) and `nodes` [{`node` [x, y, z], `count` from 1}] (no Node shared, none beyond the board; The complete example, the two slits (ALGEBRA.md row (g)). The world file `examples/events/two_slits/two_slits.json`, its thirteen NodeDetectors abridged to the first screen region and the bare region behind one gap:

A flat board of 45 x 48 Nodes (z of one Node folds), open on x and y with the faces' layer one Node deep; the wall an inner face at x = 20 with two gaps of three rows, y = 17 to 19 and 29 to 31; one packet, a packet of light (`charge`, the holder of the sign, whose record is light) along +x at the wave number pi / 4 per Link (lambda = 8), the level 1,241 at its top (1,328 before commit B, main 7756546d), the top the column x = 10 over the rows 4 to 44 with a raised cosine of six Nodes along x and two across; no body; the screen the column x = 44 in twelve regions of four rows, `screen_0` to `screen_11`, backed by the receding face of the x high side, which grows the board by sixteen layers of zeros whenever a level stands before it, up to 400 columns, so that what passes the screen leaves and never returns, and the source's face, the x low side, receding the same way, so that the wall's return leaves the declared board and nothing passes the gaps a second time (the meeting round's fix, ALGEBRA.md row (g)); the bare region `gap` of sixteen Nodes behind the lower gap, read beside the screen; 130 intervals. The universe file `examples/events/light.json`:
