Gamma = 6,000, T = 32,768 and the Link unit G = 16 at the width 63 (the hardware's integers; the amplitude bound 6,949, 9,266 under the first form's total until 2.1.0); gravity, the massless row holding the content with its three tensions at the level weight 10^6 and the vacuum content 60; the charge, the holder of the sign at the level weight 1, its quanta light; the binding holder, a gapped pair holding the content at 10^6 (the two level weights of 10^6 the LW for a light-only world, so that light's own well stays under one level); matter, one real line, laid by no body here; every row reading the two holders of the content at 1 (the charge, light's own row, none of the sign) and every write weight 1. The engine start file `examples/events/engine_start.json` is `{}`, an empty object (`{"mode": "check"}` until 2026-10-20, the cleanliness audit). The mode file `two_slits.mode.json`, written by the generator (`PYTHONPATH=src python tools/pixel_mode.py --input examples/events/two_slits/two_slits.json`), holds `world_digest` (the world's SHA-256, `7cc634ab...`), `bodies` [] and one entry under `packets`: `family` "charge", `pair` [6000, 6000], `moving` {`now`: {`at`: [344 flat indexes], `values`: [344 levels]}, `before`: the record one interval earlier, the exact lay at the paces of the declared rest, 1256 nonzero Nodes against `now`'s 344}, `amplitude` (the largest level laid under the envelope at the world file's `amplitude`, 1241 since commit B, main 7756546d and 1328 before, the design's number; the lay is the wave under its envelope at the paces of the vacuum, ALGEBRA.md #the-generator (h)) and `count` 1809 (the share in quanta the laid record reads over the board at the paces of the declared vacuum content 60, the generator's lay at those paces; 1804 at main 7756546d, the exact before level at the amplitude 1241 at the content 0; 1805 at main d00aa9e2, 1998 before the lay's fix; the books' count read once over the board). The blind expectation `expectation.json`, written by the builder before the run from the design's numbers alone and read by `tools/click_counts.py` (section 5): `verdict` "NODEDETECTOR", `node_detector` the twelve regions' names, `family` "charge", `window` [45, 130] (the intervals the screen is read over, the whole passage: nothing arrives before 45 and the run ends at 130), `across` "y" (the axis the regions are ordered along), `pattern` [1, 10] (the regions whose extrema are read), `spacing` 16, `seed` 24 (the draw's seed), `counts` [21.0, 20.0, 26.0, 15.0, 9.0, 40.0, 45.0, 16.0, 11.0, 25.0, 22.0, 25.0] (the blind row per region, the Huygens sum summed by four), `through` and `quanta` 273.0 (N's blind, the Huygens total; `counts_sum` 275.0, the twelve counts' sum by their rounding), `maxima` [2, 6, 9] (the blind row's own), `central` 6 (the blind central maximum) and `minima` [4, 8] (the blind first minima about it), `visibility` [70.0, 110.0] (the blind [most - least, most + least] at those regions), `laid` 1809 (the books' count of the lay, from the mode file, at the declared vacuum content 60; 1804 at main 7756546d, 1805 at main d00aa9e2 and 1998 before the lay's fix), `arrival` (the arrival wager in the engine's interval labels: `peak` 69, `centroid` 74 and `span` [63, 77], against the continuum's 63 and 70 and the law's real line's 68 and 73 in the physical labels, with its `status` and its `fence`), `wings` (the regions 3 and 8 at 16.4 and 12.4 against the continuum's 23.7 and 20.4, with its `status` and its `fence`) and `aside` ["gap"] (the bare region read beside the screen). Bell's gate (ALGEBRA.md #the-click-is-the-meeting; HIGHLIGHTS.md, the experiments and the worlds, **One experiment and one gate**: Bell is the engine's gate and no experiment) is four small worlds of the same kind, `examples/events/bell/bell_a_b.json`, `bell_a_b_prime.json`, `bell_a_prime_b.json` and `bell_a_prime_b_prime.json`, written by `build_world.py` from `design.json` (`--modes` writes the mode files too), one per pair of the CHSH settings (1, 0) and (1, 1) on the left and (12, 5) and (5, 12) on the right: a chain of 61 Nodes (x open, y and z of one Node folded) on the universe file `examples/events/pair.json` (the pair family `light_pair`, light's pair at the dimension [2, 1]); the pair laid as one event at the column 30, two packets of the pair family at k = pi / 4 toward +x and -x with the edge 6 at the amplitude 320 (1,328 until 2026-10-20: the bound gate at load, `loader/draw.compounding_of_the_law`, refuses the a' b and a' b' worlds there, the right's (12, 5) standing on the two-part record, 12 x 1,328 = 15,936 over A = 6,949), the lay the same on both parts; one beam to each side, the regions `left` (the columns 0 to 3) and `right` (57 to 60) backed by the receding x faces (`largest` 400, `layers` 16), each declaring its setting as its `basis`; 100 intervals, the window the whole run. Its blind expectation `expectation.json` is derived from the settings before any run and never touched after: `correlation` E = 119 / 169, -119 / 169, 120 / 169 and 120 / 169 in the CHSH `order`, `S` [478, 169], `marginal` 1 / 2 for each world, the local credits as the fence, `by_the_parts_shares` (S 238 / 169), `by_the_local_sums` (240 / 169) and `by_the_sign` (2), `of_rho` S = (238 + 240 rho) / 169, each with its `status` and its `fence`, the `rule` named (J over the window), `runs` naming the four worlds and `seed` 24; every number an exact fraction.

## 5. The output

The output handed to `Lattice` receives one dictionary per line, every line labelled by its kind (ALGEBRA.md #readings-and-measurements; a Node of the board is read only through a NodeDetector, every number of the output a click or a diagnostic labelled so). The output's words, the lines' keys and labels, the books' keys and the end's, live in `src/event_universe/reports.py` (`OUTPUT`, `BOOKS`, `END`, `reports.click`, `reports.parts`, `reports.density`, `reports.book`, `reports.end`) and nowhere else in the engine: the loader's key table holds the files' words and the reports the output's, and the engine's acts and readings hold no word (the names gate, section 8).

- The `click` line, the measurement: `event` "click", `label` "NODEDETECTOR", `interval` (the interval), `family` (a family of quanta), `node_detector` (the region's name: a declared NodeDetector's, a body's NodeDetector's, or `face`, the open faces' layer) and `inflow`, the NodeDetector's report: the net current into the region through the NodeDetector's front boundary Ports at its Nodes this interval, inward positive, in the current's units, undivided (N over the family's count wall W_c = 3 den T is the host's reading, `tools/click_counts.py`); the plain current, while the credit's books take the weighted one, each record's current times its Link's factor Q_ij in the unit G^2 (section 3, act 6, `reports.weighted`). One line per interval, family and region where it is not 0; no Node is named.
- The `parts` line, the NodeDetector's read of a family of several parts, its content the region's level sums (`reports.level_sums`, a read of the Nodes' levels packaged as the NodeDetector's line, labelled the NodeDetector's since it is read at the NodeDetector's region and nowhere else; the joint shares the credit draws from are accumulated from these sums at every interval and read at the window's close): `event` "parts", `label` "NODEDETECTOR", `interval`, `family` (a family of several parts, the pair family, the GHZ family or the spin branch's two-plane family), `node_detector` (a declared region) and `levels`, per line (one per part of a family of real-line parts, two per part of a family of planes, re then im, 2026-10-19) the signed sums [now, before] of its levels over the region's Nodes at the interval's start; one line per interval, family and declared region where a sum is not 0, no Node; emitted only where the record has several parts or several real lines (`FamilyRule.several`; a record of dimension 3 one line per real line, a plane's two lines, re and im, one line and its sense, not reported), so a world without such a family writes `click` and `density` alone; for a record of several real lines each line's share of the record is read from it as the squares of its sums over the sum of the squares. The reader pairs the parts through the root and squares (`tools/bell_gate.py`). From Bell's run `bell_a_b`: `{"event": "parts", "label": "NODEDETECTOR", "interval": 23, "family": "light_pair", "node_detector": "right", "levels": [[-1, 0], [-1, 0]]}`, the two parts equal, the determinism of equal parts.
- The `credit` line, the click written on the lattice (section 3, act 6), the one click line kind of every reader, a declared region's credit and a record's absorption and emission alike (ALGEBRA.md, The NodeDetector is one declaration kind for every experiment), the experiment's reading and nothing else (only the NodeDetector's content is read, the clicks it emits to a file, minding its clock against the board's; the inflow credited is the record's current through the region's front Ports times the Link's factor, `credit.booked` on `node_detector.weighed_currents`, the plain bilinear for a plain record and for a turned record the flux line's own with the Link pairs its step reads, as act 1 reads them, 2026-10-21): `event` "credit", `label` "NODEDETECTOR", `interval` (the board's interval, the board's clock, a diagnostic beside the reader's own), `family` (the record: the region's credited record, or the clicking record's own for a record declared a NodeDetector), `node_detector` (the region credited, or `body n`, the record declared a NodeDetector by its number in the world's order), `window` [first, last] (the board's intervals the draw ran over), `proper` (the reader's proper time at the close in whole intervals, `credit.clocked_regions`, `node_detector.clock_advanced`; the board's interval in the vacuum, less in a well), `windows` (the index of the window closed, the reader's event clock), `before` (the part the record left, by its declared name, for a record's click; None for a region's credit), `realised` (the part entered for a record's click; the port's name, `plus` or `minus`, for a region's credit of a record of several parts; None for one part), `kept` (the parts kept at the written Node for a region's credit of a record of several parts, the others ended; None otherwise), `count` (the quanta moved, 1; 0 at the null window's re-lay), `left` (the count left in the books of the record moved: the credited record's, or the family taken from or given to; None where none), `absorbed` (the family whose arriving quantum was taken, the absorption click; None otherwise) and `emitted` (the family of light a whole quantum was given to, the emission click; None otherwise); and, since the engine V2's Parts F and G and the click's plan, built on the branch claude/click-one-act (2026-10-18), `momentum` (the piece's p_a per axis in T's unit), `fan` (the record's P_a at the close), `lost` (the losses booked by name, None where nothing; "no side: booked, not faced" among them where the hole's Node is two-sided on every axis or its Port set's read coefficients sum above the wall, C6), `source` (`body n` or the lay) and `recoil` (the recoil the source owes, -p_a per axis as integers, booked and written nowhere since 2026-10-20, the source's fold per axis until then, Part G; the keys `twist`, n_a per axis, and `shortfall`, s_a = |p_a| - gain_a per axis, of 2026-10-18 gone with the fold), `bias_bound` (the close's first draw's bound by the read's bases, [the total's gcd with the bases less one, its gcd with the last base, the total], booked by `node_detector.credit_draw` and `node_detector.picked`, `features/click.bias_bound`, 2026-10-20) and, on the line of a side of a record of several parts, `order`, `scale` and `board_count` (`reports.ORDER`, `reports.SCALE`, `reports.BOARD_COUNT`); no Node (the reader writes at one Node and gives no result for one Node, the uncertainty principle; the Node written stands in the `lay` lines of the write and the `face` lines of the hole, the host's tool's). One line per click; a world without the `draw` key and without a record declared a NodeDetector writes none. From Bell's run `bell_a_b`: `{"event": "credit", "label": "NODEDETECTOR", "interval": 100, "family": "light_pair", "node_detector": "left", "window": [1, 100], "proper": 100, "windows": 1, "before": null, "realised": "minus", "kept": [1], "count": 1, "left": 197, "absorbed": null, "emitted": null}`, the left side at the setting (1, 0) realising its - port, which reads the part 2 alone (`kept` [1]), the hole at the one Node; a record's absorption carries `before` and `realised` the parts' names, `kept` None, `absorbed` the drive and `left` the drive's count after it. The null window's write, where its re-lay changed a level at a Node, is the same line labelled LATTICE throughout (no quantum passed, `count` 0), the levels in the `lay` lines beside it; a null window that changes nothing writes none.
- The `conversion` line, the click of a record converted at a declared region (section 3, act 6; `conversion.converted`, `meeting.alone`, `meeting.met`), written beside the credit line of the quantum it converts and labelled NODEDETECTOR: `event` "conversion", `interval`, `family` (the record in, its count down by one on the credit line; several records met at one Node their families' names joined with +), `node_detector` (the region by name), `window` [first, last], `into` (the families of the records out in the list's order, each up by one whole quantum laid at the credited Node by the count) and `senses` (as laid, the list's turned by the arriving record's own); no Node, the `lay` lines beside it carrying the one Node written. From examples/events/conversion/neutron_chain.json: `{"event": "conversion", "label": "NODEDETECTOR", "interval": 36, "family": "neutron", "node_detector": "target", "window": [25, 36], "into": ["proton", "electron"], "senses": [1, -1]}`.
- The `lay` line, one line of a record laid at one Node by an act from outside the Node (section 3, act 6; `act.written`), a lattice diagnostic for the host's tool and no result: `event` "lay", `label` "LATTICE", `interval`, `family` (the record's), `node_detector` None, `line` (the line's number among the family's lines), `before` and `after` ([now, before, remainder] at the Node before the lay and after it) and `node` {`label` "LATTICE", `at` [x, y, z]}; one line per line a lay changed (the absorption's and the emission's parts, the emitted quantum on light's record at each interval of its span, `emission.sourced`, the null window's re-lay). From the shelved ion's test world: `{"event": "lay", "label": "LATTICE", "interval": 1, "family": "ion", "node_detector": null, "line": 0, "before": [0, 128, 50070528000000], "after": [0, 127, 35914752000000], "node": {"label": "LATTICE", "at": [3, 3, 2]}}`, a null window's re-lay moving a level by the lay's rounding and the remainder to the lay's origin.
- The `face` line, one value presented at the Ports of one Node's set for one interval's step (section 3, act 6; `take.faces_reported`, features/click), a lattice diagnostic and no result: `event` "face", `label` "LATTICE", `interval` (the interval at whose step it is presented), `family`, `node_detector` None, `line`, `ports` (the set in Port order, the hole's the Ports the quantum entered by, from the window's inflow booked per Port, one Port per sided axis and both Ports of a tied axis, kept where the set's read coefficients sum to the wall at most and empty otherwise, the quantum then booked by the deficit path (C6, 2026-10-18 and 2026-10-19, `node_detector.arrival_ports`, `node_detector.face_ports`); a front shell Node's the Ports toward the inner shell, `front.inward`) and `value` (the arrival Rule3 read through each Port of the set in the place of the neighbour's level) and `node`; one line per face, the click's hole over its two intervals and every Node of a front's shell alike. From the same world: `{"event": "face", "label": "LATTICE", "interval": 161, "family": "fluorescence", "node_detector": null, "line": 0, "ports": [4], "value": 34, "node": {"label": "LATTICE", "at": [6, 6, 3]}}`, the counter's hole for one credited quantum at the Node it entered through, at the one Port of its set.
- The `erasure` line, the front's shell of one interval (section 3, act 6; `src/event_universe/front.py`), a lattice diagnostic for the host's tool and no result: `event` "erasure", `label` "LATTICE", `interval`, `family` (the clicked record's), `node_detector` None, `origin` [x, y, z] (the click's Node at the file's coordinates), `distance` (the shell's Link-metric distance, the intervals since the click), `nodes` (the shell's Nodes), `take` (the record's share standing on the shell as the front reaches it, in the current's units, what the shell's faces book for removal) and `unit` (the record's quantum W_rec, so that take over unit is the shell's take in quanta). One line per front per interval while its shell holds a Node. With the world's `erasure` L above 1 each shell Node is faced for L + 1 intervals at the fractions (L - 1) / L down to 1 / L of Rule3's own level, then 0 and 0 (`Face.fraction`), the last L shells before the reach falling and every shell behind them at 0; at 1 the two faces to 0 as built.
- The `density` line, a lattice reading and no measurement: `event` "density", `label` "LATTICE", `interval`, `family`, `node_detector` (a declared region; not a body's NodeDetector nor the faces' layer) and `reading`, the family's density over the region: for a family of quanta its share in quanta summed over the region, for a holder of the content the square of its time line's deviation from its rest summed; one line where it differs from the last interval's. Over a region holding a frozen Node (every Link pace 0, where the share is not read and no number is invented, ALGEBRA.md #the-count-is-the-records-share) `reading` is None and the line carries `well`, the frozen Nodes' wells D div T summed, a key of that line alone.
- The books, `Lattice.books()`, a lattice diagnostic: per family of quanta `share` (its share summed over the lattice in the current's units), `quanta` (the same over the family's quantum, `derived.count_wall`: largest_below(9 T^2 (den^2 - num^2)) by `core/paces.sine_den`, the engine's one integer sine, W_c sin omega_0 to the unit, for a family with a gap, W_c = 3 den T for a massless one), `count` (the record's count in the credit's books), `deficit` (the quanta taken from the record and written nowhere less the quanta given into it and laid nowhere, the undepleted beam, `credit.Books.deficits`: the board's share in quanta stands above the books' count by the deficit, the three read side by side at every reading), `drift` (the share against the one the world started with: Rule3's own rounding and the paces' own change over the run), `pace` (the least Node pace p_i = p_0^2 / Gamma of the final state) and `frozen` (the count of its frozen Nodes; `share`, `quanta` and `drift` None while there is one). From the ion-like test world's run of 170 intervals (`tests/test_the_lay.py`, the drive taken three times and written nowhere): `"strong_drive": {"share": 862280640000, "quanta": 1462, "count": 1461, "deficit": 3, "drift": -1306800000, "pace": 6000, "frozen": 0}`, the drift about two quanta of the share, so that `quanta` reads the count plus the deficit within it.
- The end, `Lattice.ended`: None while the run goes; where the front stands on the layer before a receding face of an axis grown to its largest size, the lawful end named, `interval`, `axis`, `side` and `largest`, the run stopping there rather than reflecting.

`tools/run_inputs.py` (`PYTHONPATH=src python tools/run_inputs.py --out <folder> --jobs 4 <world>.json ...`) runs each world in its own process and writes `<folder>/<world>.output.json`: `input` (the world file's name), `verdict` (`LAWFUL`, or `REFUSED` with `reason`, the loader's, the guard's or the gate's refusal by name at load, or a refusal inside the run with its interval), `intervals` (the intervals run), `ended` (None, or the end's four keys), `books` and `lines` (every `click`, `parts`, `credit`, `density`, `erasure`, `lay` and `face` line in the order they were written); the output carries no time. The two slits' run: `{"input": "two_slits.json", "verdict": "LAWFUL", "intervals": 130, "ended": null, ...}` with 1,209 `click` lines, 134 `density` lines and 278 `credit` lines (the run on main aea3e05) at the interval 130, the window's end.

The readers, both host tools that compute nothing the files do not declare:

- `tools/click_counts.py` (`PYTHONPATH=src python tools/click_counts.py --world <world>.json --output <world>.output.json --expectation <expectation>.json`) reads a screen's credit from an output file against the blind expectation file: the expectation's `node_detector` (a list; every declared region where it names none), `family`, `window` [first, last] and `across` place one reporter per region at the least coordinate of its Nodes on that axis; what each region saw is its `click` lines' inflows summed over the window (`seen`), floored at 0 (`floored` says whether any was; the credit's floor, the NodeDetector's declaration, ALGEBRA.md #the-click-is-the-meeting), N (`quanta`) the total over W_c to the nearest whole, (total + W_c div 2) div W_c, beside `blind_quanta`; two rows named for what they are: `rounded_shares`, N times the shares apportioned by the largest remainders (a tie of remainders broken by the lower index), the expectation and no sample, and `clicks`, the quanta the draw credited inside the run, per region its `credit` lines' counts within the window (the click written on the lattice with the world's declared seed and generator, section 3, act 6; one quantum to one NodeDetector; None for a world declaring no draw), the tool drawing nothing; the tie rule and the draw's generator are the NodeDetector's declaration, as the window and the seed are, and no line of the law; each row with its `maxima` and `minima` within `pattern` (`extrema`, the one rule of the builder's blind row and the reader's rows, which `examples/events/two_slits/build_world.py` imports: a position above its left neighbour and at or above its right is a maximum, below its left and at or below its right a minimum, and the row's ends are neither), its `visibility` at the blind central maximum (the expectation's `central`; the middle of its `maxima` where it names none) against the blind first minima as [most - least, most + least], its `deviation` from the blind row's shares over the total as [numerator, denominator] and its `wings`, the counts at the wings' regions; the `blind` row beside them with its own extrema, visibility, wings and `sum`; `arrival`, the screen's inflow over every interval of the window in the engine's labels, the negative ones included (the law names the peak and the centroid over the window and excludes nothing; the precursors at the front of the two slits' run are negative), its `peak` interval (the first at the largest inflow), its `centroid` as an exact fraction and its half-maximum `span`, beside `blind_arrival`; `laid` (the expectation's) and `elsewhere` (`laid` less N, the part that left the board elsewhere); and the bare regions named in `aside` with what they saw. The two slits' reading at the seed 24 over the whole passage, the window [45, 130] (the run on main aea3e05, at the lay before the fix): `quanta` 278 (the blind 273; the law's real line over the same passage 277.8), `rounded_shares` [20, 20, 27, 17, 9, 40, 47, 15, 12, 27, 21, 23] with `maxima` [2, 6, 9], `minima` [4, 8, 10], `visibility` [73, 115] (the blind [70, 110]), `deviation` [1834, 38225] and `wings` [17, 12] (the blind 16.4 and 12.4), `clicks` [16, 17, 33, 12, 13, 42, 44, 10, 11, 21, 32, 27] (the 278 quanta the draw credited at the interval 130, the window's end), `arrival` `peak` 69, `centroid` 73.73 (an exact fraction in the file) and `span` [63, 77] (the blind 69, 74 and [63, 77]), `laid` 1998, `elsewhere` 1720, `floored` false, `aside` {"gap": {"seen": -337470000, "quanta": 0}} (the gap's window sum under 0 by a fraction of a quantum, floored) (ALGEBRA.md row (g)); at the lay without the uniform mode (main d00aa9e2; the folder's `blind_and_reading.md`, the reading at fb2c14c, the seed 24): `quanta` 270 (the blind 273.0), the rounded shares [20, 19, 26, 16, 9, 39, 46, 14, 12, 26, 21, 22] (LATTICE), `clicks` [28, 15, 24, 18, 7, 37, 44, 13, 13, 25, 24, 22], the maxima [2, 6, 9] the blind's, the clicks' minima [1, 4, 7] against the shares' [1, 4, 8, 10], `visibility` [68, 108] (the shares' [71, 113]), the wings [18, 13], the gap 0, the arrival's peak 69, centroid 73.8 and span [63, 77] (the blind's), `laid` 1805 and `elsewhere` the laid less N; at commit B, main 7756546d, the gate pin of `tests/test_the_draw.py` until the pick from the high bits, a lattice diagnostic read at that commit and labelled so: `quanta` 284 at the seed 24, the clicks [20, 18, 24, 18, 11, 37, 61, 10, 12, 19, 17, 37], the chi-squares at the seeds 24 and 25 [20.6, 4.7] (the sum 25.3 inside Born's band 8.6 to 42.8), the seeds 26 to 35 at N = 284 with the mean chi-square 12.3, `laid` 1804; at the pick from the high bits (K1, the clean main) the same lay, the gate pin since, a lattice diagnostic read at that commit and labelled so: `quanta` 284 at the seed 24, the clicks [21, 22, 27, 16, 8, 36, 56, 11, 12, 27, 23, 25], the chi-squares at the seeds 24 and 25 [3.4, 12.5] (the sum 16.0 inside Born's band), the seeds 26 to 35 at N = 284 with the mean chi-square 10.0, `laid` 1804; at the declared vacuum content 60 with the packet laid at the content-0 band (main 126b1644, the gate pin there): `quanta` 290 at the seed 24, the clicks [21, 22, 28, 17, 8, 39, 57, 11, 12, 26, 24, 25], the chi-squares at the seeds 24 and 25 [3.1, 11.2], `laid` 1804 (the books at the world's paces reading 1844, the lay's band and the step's paces apart); at the packet laid at the declared rest's paces (the gate pin since), a lattice diagnostic read at that change and labelled so: `quanta` 285 at the seed 24, the clicks [22, 21, 27, 16, 9, 36, 56, 11, 12, 26, 24, 25], the chi-squares at the seeds 24 and 25 [3.7, 12.4] (the sum 16.1 inside Born's band), `laid` 1809, the books' count at the world's paces.
- `tools/bell_gate.py` (`PYTHONPATH=src python tools/bell_gate.py --expectation examples/events/bell/expectation.json --outputs runs/bell/bell_a_b.output.json runs/bell/bell_a_b_prime.output.json runs/bell/bell_a_prime_b.output.json runs/bell/bell_a_prime_b_prime.output.json`, the four worlds' output files by name; the GHZ gate the same with `examples/events/ghz/expectation.json` and `runs/ghz/ghz_x_y_y.output.json runs/ghz/ghz_y_x_y.output.json runs/ghz/ghz_y_y_x.output.json runs/ghz/ghz_x_x_x.output.json`) reads a gate from its four worlds' output files against the blind (ALGEBRA.md #the-click-is-the-meeting, the pair's form and the GHZ gate): each world found by its output's `input` name among the expectation's `runs`; each side's two ports from its declared `basis` (p, q) and `pattern`, e_k(+) = alpha_k p + beta_k q and e_k(-) = alpha_k (-q) + beta_k p (the pair's (p, q) and (-q, p)), refused by name where the two are not orthogonal with equal norms or the pattern's length is not the family's parts; the joint share of every combination of ports, one per side, accumulated over the window on both members of the level pair from the sides' `parts` lines, J = SUM over the window of (SUM_k c_k PROD_sides now_k)^2 + (SUM_k c_k PROD_sides before_k)^2 with c_k the product over the sides of e_k at their ports, a sum of squares and never negative; per world `joint` (the J's) and `shares` (the J's over their sum), `correlation` E_n (the shares signed by the product of the ports' signs over their sum; for two sides (J_++ + J_-- - J_+- - J_-+) over the four), `marginals` per side (its + shares over the sum), `sub_correlations` over every subset of two or more sides below n (the GHZ's three pairwise E), `drawn`, the combination of ports the draw realised inside the run, one port per side from the `credit` lines (the click; None for a world declaring no draw), the reader drawing nothing, and `quanta`, the sides' inflow over W_c; the four worlds' E combined with the expectation's `combination`, its name and its signs over the expectation's `order`: `S` = E(a, b) - E(a, b') + E(a', b) + E(a', b') for Bell (the signs 1, -1, 1, 1) and `M` = E(x, y, y) + E(y, x, y) + E(y, y, x) - E(x, x, x) for the GHZ (1, 1, 1, -1); beside it from the same reports the local credits as the fence under the same name, `S_by_the_parts_shares` or `M_by_the_parts_shares` (each part's share alone), `S_by_the_local_sums` (the product of the sides' own squared sums) and `S_by_the_sign` (each side's larger port, 0 at a tie), and per world the diagnostics `mismatch` {products, ratios, rho}, the parts' accumulated cross-side products, their ratios to the first part's (the pair's r) and the pair's 2 M_1 M_2 / (M_1^2 + M_2^2), labelled LATTICE; every number an exact fraction. Bell's four worlds' reading: `correlation` 119 / 169, -119 / 169, 120 / 169, 120 / 169, `S` [478, 169] and every marginal [1, 2], the blind's exactly; `S_by_the_parts_shares` [238, 169], `S_by_the_local_sums` [240, 169], `S_by_the_sign` [2, 1]; the ratio and rho 1; the pairs the draw realised (-, -), (-, +), (-, -), (-, -); about 99 quanta per side. The GHZ's four worlds' reading: the eight `shares` 0 at an even number of - ports and 1 / 4 at an odd on the three worlds with one x and 1 / 4 at an even and 0 at an odd on (x, x, x), `correlation` -1, -1, -1, 1, `M` [-4, 1], every marginal [1, 2] and every pairwise E [0, 1], the blind's exactly; `M_by_the_parts_shares`, `M_by_the_local_sums` and `M_by_the_sign` [-1, 1]; the ratios 1; the triples the draw realised (+, +, -), (+, +, -), (+, +, -), (+, +, +); about 956 quanta on the sides a and b and 811 on the side c. The reading's form (2026-10-19, the cold reader's finding, no change of the tool): `tools/bell_gate.py` reads a parts line one row per part (`credited`), the form of a family of real lines, right for the pair and the GHZ, and would misread a family of planes, whose parts line holds two rows per part, the part 0's im line read as the part 1; the plane's reading pairs the two rows (re, im) of each part as the engine's joint credit did until 2026-10-20, the tool `tools/spin_gate.py`, built on this branch (2026-10-19) with the three declared words entered on the owner's word of 2026-10-19, which reads the family's declared sign from the loaded world and forms the meeting's amplitude over the ordered pairs of parts with the sign where it is antisymmetric, the determinant of the two sides' parts, and over the parts alone where it is symmetric or undeclared, two sides and more refused by name, the local credits by the parts' shares, by the local sums and by the sign kept as the fences.
- `tools/spin_gate.py` (`PYTHONPATH=src python tools/spin_gate.py --expectation <folder>/expectation.json --outputs runs/<folder>/*.output.json`), the joint-share reader for a gate of a record of planes, bell_gate's reading in the plane's form: a part of a plane family holds two lines, re and im, so the region's `parts` line holds 2 x parts rows, the part k's pair on the rows 2 k and 2 k + 1, and the meeting's share is J = SUM over the window and both members of |SUM_k c_k PROD_sides psi^s_k|^2, the product over the sides a plane's product and the square the pair's norm; the side's setting read as the loader reads it, `axis` for a record of planes and `basis` otherwise, the ports' arithmetic one (`loader/draw.ports_of`); the family's declared sign under exchange read from the loaded world (`FamilyRule.exchange`) and the shares built in the sign's form (`credited`, `terms_of`): over the parts for the symmetric or undeclared record, over the ordered pairs (k, l), k != l, with sign(l - k) for the antisymmetric, the determinant of the two sides' parts, two sides and more refused by name, the form named in each world's `exchange` and `form`; the three local credits (by the parts' shares, by the local sums, by the sign) the fences as they stand whatever the sign; `at_equal_parts(ports, exchange)` the blind's numbers in the same form for a folder's builder, the crossed pattern's numbers at the plain identity on both sides under the antisymmetric sign.
- `tools/standing_lay.py` (`PYTHONPATH=src python tools/standing_lay.py --input <world>.json --record <family> --source <family> --sense -1 [--lay standing --level 1s | --lay gaussian --sigma 4]`), the generator of the standing lay of a charged record in the well of the holder of the sign, a lattice reading and no measurement, holding no law but Rule3's own lines and the files' declarations and naming no family, no world and no number of the law: the sources laid as the generator's one-Node pixel record of their declared count at their own pair (2 A^2 sin omega_s = T per quantum, the engine's search and division acts, tools/pixel_mode.py's `pixel_record` mirrored, `pixel_entry`); the well read off the engine's own start with the record's levels at 0 (every row of the holder but the record's own, `loader/derived.row_of`), U_i = -k_r h_i / Gamma; the standing level the eigenvector of Rule3's own standing line at every Node from the engine's rule integers of the record's read (`node.rule_of` on `Lattice.read`: R_ij / w on the Links, S_i / w at the Node, the paces of the start; `rule_arrays`), 2 cos(Omega + U_i) phi_i = SUM_j (R_ij / w) phi_j + (S_i / w) phi_i, symmetrised by the law's weight 1 / p_i^2 where the paces differ (`operator`: B_ij = (R_ij / w) (p_j / p_i) / sqrt(cos U_i cos U_j), B_ii = S_i / w + 2 sin Omega sin U_i / cos U_i, phi_i = p_i chi_i / sqrt(cos U_i)), the vacuum's h = num / (3 den) and S_i = 0 the special case where the record reads no holder of the content; the eigensolver started from one seeded vector at one tolerance (`START_SEED`, `TOLERANCE`), so the lay and the mode file's digest are the same on every run; the level named by its kind and its rank (s, p, d by the monopole and the dipole), Omega iterated to the level's own fixed point, the lay in the engine's phase convention (now = A phi cos U, im_now = -A phi sin U, before = A phi cos Omega, im_before = A phi sin Omega); the mode file beside the world and `<world>.standing.json` with the tool's readings (the well along an axis, the rule's integers over the wall, Omega, the binding, A, the profile, the Wronskian, the eight levels' Omega and kinds); `--lay gaussian` the free lump, the control that does not stand; the standing lay normalised to the charge of its count, W = count T / 2, A = sqrt(T / (2 SUM phi_i^2 sin(Omega + U_i))) (2026-10-22).
- `tools/frame_of.py` (`PYTHONPATH=src python tools/frame_of.py --input <world>.json --light <family> [--lifetime N] [--width N] [--weight N] [--root <dir>]`): the bound record's declaration typed into the mode file's `instrument` key from the frame's reading of the standing record (ALGEBRA.md, The click writes on the lattice (3), the clause of 2026-10-21): the levels by name from the fit with the count in the part standing, the transition to the light's family at its weight (the record's read weight of its holder where none is given) and resonance, the rate at the lifetime given, the region the laid part's whole support joined through the six Ports, with the shares as weights; the engine's loader refuses a defective declaration by name (`loader/instrument.py`).
- `tools/body_rest.py` (`PYTHONPATH=src python tools/body_rest.py [--intervals N] [--reach R] [--expectation <expectation>.json] [--output <world>.output.json] <world>.json ...`), the families round's reader of a body's rest, a lattice reading labelled so and no measurement: a world of one or two bodies is loaded as the runner loads it and stepped by the engine's own step over the window (the world's `intervals`, or `--intervals`), and at the window's ends every body is read from its record and the rows it sources: its centre (the declared Node of its largest count), the three levels of its record about one interval at the centre, [before, now, next], and their ratio (next + before) / now = 2 cos omega_b as an exact fraction, the content its record reads at the centre (`content_at_centre`) and the one-Node line's value from it (`one_node_line_at_centre`), its Wronskian at the centre where it is a plane, its share in quanta at the centre and summed over its region (for two bodies the half of the board nearer its centre along the axis joining the centres, for one the whole board), the centroid of its share and the share-weighted second moment about it (the rms radius squared), its well over the region (the form D = now^2 - next x before summed and divided by T, in quanta), its record's levels along each axis from the centre outward to `--reach` Links with the ratios of successive levels, and every held row's time level along the same axes; for two bodies the separation of the centroids along the joining axis at the ends and its change; the books at the end and, beside their drift, the two terms of its identity summed over every interval stepped (ALGEBRA.md, The share's change is the currents; The conserved form), the work term of L510 (the share of each step's pair at the paces after the step less the same pair at the paces the step read) and Rule3's remainder term (the rounding of the level, by the division act at every Node), each in the current's units and in quanta, the drift the two plus the currents through the faces to the per-Node division's rounding; where a receding face grows the lattice during the interval read, the arrays kept at the interval's start are grown with it and the regions read at the shape after the step. With `--output` (once per file) a run's `density` lines are read over the window for the regions and the family the expectation names under `field` (every region and family without them), each region's least and largest reading with their intervals and the count of its local maxima; with `--expectation` the blind's `reading`, `blind`, `fence` and `status` are printed beside the readings. The tool compares nothing, holds no number of the law and writes nothing to the engine.
- `tools/body_standing.py` (`PYTHONPATH=src python tools/body_standing.py [--intervals N] [--beat-period P] [--expectation <expectation>.json] <world>.json ...`), the standing world's reader, a lattice reading labelled so and no measurement: a world of one body stepped by the engine's own step over the window, and at the body's declared Nodes, exact fractions of the engine's integers, the reads of the design, with reads 2 and 3 restated for a real mode. (1) The centroid of its share along each axis at the window's ends and its drift (the centroid along each axis, the density summed over the cross-section at each x). (2) The deviation over the body of the form D_i = now^2 - next x before, the law's own static quantity per Node (A_i^2 sin^2 omega exactly for one real mode), rho_D^2 = SUM (D_i(k) - D_i(0))^2 / SUM D_i(0)^2 at every form centre k (the form at k from z_(k - 1), z_k, z_(k + 1), the lay's own form the reference, the window's last centre one before its end) and at the quarters, rho_D itself [rho_D T, T] by the search on the share reading at the scale T (`rule3.largest_below`, as the tool computes it), with the series' envelope (its local maxima and their spacings) at the interval scale and over the means of successive windows of one period, the beat of a second laid mode read there and a rest short of its static line ringing in the board's lowest mode named if present (closed by the refined rest of the fix round, section 3), and with `--beat-period` the Fourier line of the series at that period in floats, a diagnostic labelled so (the beat's weight, reported and not blinded), with the series less the line at the quarters, the walk's part; beside it the share's deviation rho_s^2 = SUM (s_n - s_0)^2 / SUM s_0^2 on the share per Node at the paces of the read, the design's convention kept and labelled, the real mode's own breathing at 2 omega, at the quarters and with its series' maxima and spacings (`breathing`). (3) The rotation over windows of whole periods, 2 cos omega_read = SUM over the window of SUM over the body of z_t (z_(t + 1) + z_(t - 1)) / the same double sum of z_t^2, the period in whole intervals from the mode file's clock pair by the rotation's own recurrence (2 cos omega = clock[0] / clock[1], no angle and no root), exact as a fraction per successive window and over the whole run, the windows' least, largest and spread and the window series' envelope; beside it the per-interval read cos omega_read = SUM z_t (z_(t + 1) + z_(t - 1)) / (2 SUM z_t^2), labelled near-singular twice a period for a real mode, its least and largest over the intervals where the record's weight 2 SUM z_t^2 is at least half its largest and the window's accumulated ratio. (4) The cube's 48 images (the cube's symmetry theorem as a lattice diagnostic, the body round's part 2): at the lay and after every interval every line of every family against its 48 images about the board's centre, the scalars equal to the bit with their remainders and write remainders, a holder's tension lines carried as a tensor's diagonal and its odd lines signed with the axis with the remainders complemented where signed (`records.complement`), the interval through which the 48 held and the first departure named (the standing world's whole run of 1000 intervals to the bit under the write's one rounding, a reading of `tools/body_standing.py` by name and no test, the 48 asserted as a unit on the closed cube in `tests/test_the_node.py`; through 515 under the three roundings before the fix round). The books at the end; with an expectation file the blind and the body's own numbers from the lay printed beside the readings, compared by hand.
- `tools/meeting_trials.py` (`PYTHONPATH=src python tools/meeting_trials.py --world <world>.json --design <design>.json [--expectation <expectation>.json]`), the trials of a world of records declared NodeDetectors: the world run once per seed of the design's `seeds` from the same lay, each record's generator at a state of its own from the seed, over the design's `trial_intervals` (the world's `intervals`, the run's length, where the design names none); per record the fraction of trials ending in each part (the books' part as the last write left it), the clicks per kind, and over two records the fractions of trials with an absorption at one alone, at both and at neither with alpha = P(both) / (P(A) P(B)); the blind printed beside where given.
- `tools/back_in_time.py` (`PYTHONPATH=src python tools/back_in_time.py --intervals 40 <world>.json`), the back-in-time gate of section 3, prints `{"verdict": "MATCH", "intervals": 40, "interval": 1, "ended": null}` on the two slits' world and, with `--intervals 100`, on each of Bell's four worlds and, with `--intervals 60`, on each of the GHZ's four, or MISS with the first interval, array and Node that differ.

## 6. How to run a world

1. Write a world file with its bodies declared on their Nodes and its packets by their keys, naming a universe file and `examples/events/engine_start.json` (section 4); or build one of the two gates' worlds from its design file, `PYTHONPATH=src python examples/events/two_slits/build_world.py` (the world, its mode file and its blind expectation into `examples/events/two_slits/`, byte for byte the files there) and `PYTHONPATH=src python examples/events/bell/build_world.py --modes` (the four worlds, their mode files and the blind expectation into `examples/events/bell/`), and the GHZ gate's by `PYTHONPATH=src python examples/events/ghz/build_world.py --modes` (its four worlds, their mode files and its blind into `examples/events/ghz/`); `--folder <folder>` writes elsewhere.
2. Lay its bodies and packets: `PYTHONPATH=src python tools/pixel_mode.py --input <world>.json` (its inputs the folder's design file's beside the world, `design_entry`: the count `quanta`, the sense per body `senses` and the one-Node declarations `pixels`, its world's entry or the design's own, nothing on the command line, `--sense` and `--pixel` refused by name) finds every body's fixed point in the rows its family reads (the held rows at the rests the engine's own start lays under its record, `start_content` calling `booked_sources` and `held_rests`, the one act with `Lattice.start`: the record's form as the held write books it over the write's wall; the declared count the seed of the first pass alone), rewrites the bodies' Nodes and counts (the record's share in quanta at the paces the engine reads at the start, `read_at_the_start` by the same act, so that the gate's reading is the declaration within the rounding), lays every packet as the wave under its envelope with its level one interval earlier (ALGEBRA.md #the-generator (h); a wave at p = 0 is refused by name by the generator itself before any lay, `wave_of`, naming the packet and the wave, as the loader refuses it) and writes `<world>.mode.json` beside it; a sense, one per body in the world's order (the design's `senses`), lays a body of dimension 2 rotating (its second level pair the record a quarter period on, laid inside the iteration so that the holder of the sign rests with the content; refused by name on a family of dimension 1), two bodies or more in two passes; the search's memory is bounded by a digest per state. The generator's input is the design's count (`designed_quanta`: the folder's `design.json` beside the world, its world's entry `quanta` or the design's own; a new body declared on one Node its declared count), never the world's declared Nodes' counts, which are its output, the engine's reading of the lay before: a body declared on its Nodes without the design's key is refused by name, the folder and the key named, and no mode file beside the world stands for the design; its loop (`body_fixed_point`) returns the record, the content and the counts of one round, when the content returns itself by the start's own rule (`returned`, one unit per division act composed) and the counts within the rounding; the record is the top mode of Rule3's read act on the board as declared (`top_mode` over `on_the_board`, 0 beyond a face as the engine reads it, never cut at the body's region: a mode of the cut operator is no mode of the board's), laid at its peak with before = next = the read div 2; and every lay is checked standing one step in its own start (`standing_check`: the laid world stepped once by Rule3 in the content `Lattice.start` lays, the rotation (next + before) / now over the body's Nodes one number on every level pair within one unit of 1 / |now| per act composed, four, else refused by name with the centre's rotation, the median and the mode file's clock printed). A world declaring the lay `fixed_point` (section 4, the key `lay`) is laid by `unit_fixed_point` instead: the same map without the counts' half step, iterated until the record's two levels and the content repeat the pass before within the declared stop at every Node inside the declared passes, the trajectory printed per pass and written to the mode file, the body refused by name with its trajectory where the passes run out; a world declaring `lay.seed` `compact` has its first pass laid by `compact_seed`, the design's count at the seed Node and the Nodes of its six Ports in the proportion the world's `profile` declares (the Node itself through a folded Port, nothing beyond a face), where the one-Node seed spreads the count over the smallest cube with a positive pace (`spread`), the seed of the first pass alone and no result, recorded in the mode file by name. A body of a plane that a holder of the sign turns (ALGEBRA.md, The atom is a bound body of the holder of the sign) is laid as the turned top mode (`turned_mode`, the atom's round): the power iteration of Rule3's read with the arrivals turned by the Link angles (the odd lines' numerators as the first build's `plane.turned_ports` turned them) and the Node by the time angle, z <- (M z)_i wall_i div numerator_i + z_i with numerator_i / wall_i = 2 cos(Omega - theta_i) the standing condition at the clock pair (the turn's own rationals, tan(theta_i / 2) = n_i / 2 Gamma) and the shift by one unit keeping the band's bottom modes from the iteration, the clock re-read from the mode's own ratio until it repeats, in the content holders' paces alone (the holder under the rotation entering no pace), its angles the engine's own start's under the other bodies' laid records (`start_content`, `node.turning`), its two lines laid rotating in the body's sense at that clock (`quarter_turned`), its region the board as kept, where its record stands, and the standing check against the lay's own clock with the two turns' six shears allowed (the first build's generator; its turned path is removed on V2, 1723e28ff); a record of the law's count 1, whose share at every Node is below a quantum, is scaled to one quantum over its region by the summed share and keeps its declared Node and count, which the gate admits within its rounding; a body numbered in the design's `pixels` is laid as the one-Node record of its quanta at its declared Node in its sense, a declaration by name and no fixed point (`pixel_record`: 2 A^2 sin omega_s = T per quantum, the level before the band's rest rotation, the Wronskian sense x T / 2; the atom's nucleus), its Nodes no other body's cut and no sharing check; the generator's arrays are of the kind the universe's width chooses, as the loader's are (`loader.world.kind_of`; Python's integers above 63 bits, exact and slow). A world of packets alone (the two gates' worlds) needs the generator for the mode file all the same.
3. Run it headless: `PYTHONPATH=src python tools/run_inputs.py --out runs/first --jobs 4 <world>.json`, one output file per world, one summary line printed per world; the two slits' world runs in a few seconds on one core (its 130 intervals, the board grown to 269 columns by the end of the run, the file's largest 400), Bell's four chains in seconds each (100 intervals, the chain grown to 221 Nodes by the end, the file's largest 400), the GHZ's four square boards of 33 x 33 Nodes in a few seconds each (60 intervals, the board grown at its four receding faces).
4. Read the verdict first: `REFUSED` names the key, the value, the guard or the gate that refused; `LAWFUL` carries the lines and the books (section 5).
5. Gate it: `PYTHONPATH=src python tools/back_in_time.py --intervals 40 <world>.json` says MATCH on the two slits' world, printing `{"verdict": "MATCH", "intervals": 40, "interval": 1, "ended": null}` (a run on main 637a062), with `--intervals 100` on each of Bell's four worlds and with `--intervals 60` on each of the GHZ's four, and on the reader worlds across their clicks (`--intervals 60` on `examples/events/bell_singlet/born_a_b.json`, across its clicks; the Zeno and the anticoincidence worlds left the tree 2026-10-20), the faces presented from the `face` lines and the lays undone from the `lay` lines (on the fix round's engine, the nine gate worlds MATCH at 130, 100 and 60 and every number is unmoved, N 278, S 478 / 169, M -4); a reading is read only on MATCH. The click's checks, each one test function: the gate worlds bit for bit through the one act with MATCH on each (`tests/test_the_meeting.py::test_the_meeting_is_the_pairing_through_the_root_and_the_engine_implements_it_exactly`, `::test_the_ghz_gate_pairs_four_parts_through_the_root_on_three_sides_exactly`); the credit at one Node, the inverse across the clicks and the count-0 record credited never with no draw consumed (`::test_the_click_is_written_at_one_node_and_the_board_is_exact_between_clicks`); the absorption, the emission and the null window at one Node, the front's theorem, the back-in-time gate across every act from the lines on the shelved ion's test world and the two shipped reader worlds with the start returned, the booking identity per act at every step (the conversion's list through the one act and the generator's consumption asserted to 2026-10-20, gone with their code), the face lines equal to the books' values and the one-in-flight limit's absence by name (`tests/test_the_node_detector.py::test_a_record_declared_a_reader_over_two_nodes_takes_gives_and_stays`, with `tests/laws.py::booked`); the share identity on the gate worlds and the hand worlds (`tests/test_the_node.py::test_the_interval_on_a_closed_cube_conserves_the_count_keeps_the_48_returns`).
6. Read the two slits' credit: `PYTHONPATH=src python tools/click_counts.py --world examples/events/two_slits/two_slits.json --output runs/first/two_slits.output.json --expectation examples/events/two_slits/expectation.json`, the numbers of section 5; read Bell's gate: `PYTHONPATH=src python tools/run_inputs.py --out runs/bell --jobs 4 examples/events/bell/bell_a_b.json examples/events/bell/bell_a_b_prime.json examples/events/bell/bell_a_prime_b.json examples/events/bell/bell_a_prime_b_prime.json` (the four worlds by name: a glob over the folder would take the mode files too, which the runner refuses and the reader cannot load) then `PYTHONPATH=src python tools/bell_gate.py --expectation examples/events/bell/expectation.json --outputs runs/bell/bell_a_b.output.json runs/bell/bell_a_b_prime.output.json runs/bell/bell_a_prime_b.output.json runs/bell/bell_a_prime_b_prime.output.json`, S = 478 / 169 and the marginal 1 / 2 exactly, the ratio and rho 1; read the GHZ gate: `PYTHONPATH=src python tools/run_inputs.py --out runs/ghz --jobs 4 examples/events/ghz/ghz_x_y_y.json examples/events/ghz/ghz_y_x_y.json examples/events/ghz/ghz_y_y_x.json examples/events/ghz/ghz_x_x_x.json` then `PYTHONPATH=src python tools/bell_gate.py --expectation examples/events/ghz/expectation.json --outputs runs/ghz/ghz_x_y_y.output.json runs/ghz/ghz_y_x_y.output.json runs/ghz/ghz_y_y_x.output.json runs/ghz/ghz_x_x_x.output.json`, M = -4, every marginal 1 / 2 and every pairwise E 0 exactly (`tests/test_the_meeting.py` runs both gates' four worlds from their design files). Beside N the reader prints the photon count at the light's own frequency, N over sin omega with cos omega = (cos(pi p / q) + 2) / 3 for the laid wave [p, q] (645 at N = 278 and k = pi / 4), a lattice reading labelled so and no result: the screen's regions declare the band's top, so N counts the energy in units of T and the laid light's own quanta are N over sin omega.
7. Run and read the shelved ion's telegraph: `PYTHONPATH=src python tools/pixel_mode.py --input examples/events/shelved_ion/shelved_ion.json` lays the two drives (the ion, laid in its parts, takes no mode entry) and the same for `shelved_ion_control.json`; `PYTHONPATH=src python tools/run_inputs.py --out runs/ion examples/events/shelved_ion/shelved_ion.json examples/events/shelved_ion/shelved_ion_control.json` runs both (a run refused inside by the amplitude bound keeps its lines to that interval); `python tools/telegraph.py --output runs/ion/shelved_ion.output.json --expectation examples/events/shelved_ion/expectation.json` reads the counts per bin, the periods, the dark fraction, the histogram, the switches, the returns and the jumps beside the blind's rows.
8. The Zeno world and the anticoincidence world, their builders and their readings, left the tree 2026-10-20 at the ninth with their folders (#2156; section 4, the folders' history); `tools/meeting_trials.py` stands and reads the resonance world below (its coincidence rows derived from the records' count, `coincidence_rows`: the absorptions at every record alone and at every pair and no other, at neither, and alpha per pair; for two records A alone, B alone, both, neither and alpha, a world of three records its three singles and three pairs). Build, lay and run the which-way world: `PYTHONPATH=src python examples/events/which_way/build_world.py --modes` writes the three worlds, their mode files and the blind, `PYTHONPATH=src python tools/run_inputs.py --out runs/way examples/events/which_way/which_way.json examples/events/which_way/one_gap.json examples/events/which_way/two_gaps.json` runs them, and the clicks per region are the `credit` lines per NodeDetector, read beside `expectation.json` as `blind_and_reading.md` reads them. Build, lay and run the resonance world, test (vi): `PYTHONPATH=src python examples/events/resonance/build_world.py --modes` writes `resonant.json` and `detuned.json` with their mode files and the blind, `PYTHONPATH=src python tools/meeting_trials.py --world examples/events/resonance/resonant.json --design examples/events/resonance/design.json --expectation examples/events/resonance/expectation.json` reads the taker's absorptions over the design's seeds, `PYTHONPATH=src python examples/events/resonance/read_world.py` reads both worlds over the seeds under the dark grain (per seed the emission's interval, the lay lines, the far Node's cosine on its plateau and the light's count at the span's end, lattice readings labelled so, with the absorptions), as `tests/test_the_draw.py` reads them over the first seeds and `blind_and_reading.md` records them.
9. The rotation round's look (the like-or-unlike worlds of `examples/events/like_or_unlike/`, two bodies of the charged family on a chain under the holder of the sign's act `rotation`, with their uncharged controls, read by `tools/body_drift.py`) left the repository by decision, the six charged worlds refused at load and the three uncharged ones the control of nothing; its universe file stands at `examples/events/turning.json` for the rotation's tests, and its readings stand in the repository's history by name.
10. See it, a diagnostic: `PYTHONPATH=src python tools/look/record.py <world>.json --intervals N` is the host's reader, which steps the world by the engine's own step and writes every family's arrays per interval (a family of quanta's two levels now, its count as the record's share in quanta, a reading of the record, its share over the lattice in the current's units, the books' per interval, the form the interval booked and the least Node pace of its read; a held family's level and its further parts), each frame over the lattice's shape at that interval with its `shape` and `offset` (the layers grown before the origin, every Node of the frame named at the file's coordinates), the `click` lines, the bodies' Nodes derived, the world's declared numbers (the inner faces with their gaps and the receding faces among them), the end where the run ended at a receding face's largest size and the books to `<world>.look.json`, labelled "lattice reading" and holding no number of its own; frame 0 is the world as laid, its counts the share the gate admitted the bodies' declarations within. With `--every K` the look keeps frame 0, the frame of every K-th interval and the last frame stepped (1, the default, every frame), each kept frame the full look's frame at that interval bit for bit with its own `click` and `parts` lines, so that a long run's look fits one page; the intervals between the kept frames stand in no frame, the readings read a full look recorded locally and not shipped, and a page of a strided look takes its measurement from the run's output file (`--output`); the look names its stride in `every` and the summary line its frames. `PYTHONPATH=src python tools/look/page.py <world>.look.json [--blind <expectation>.json]` writes the one page that shows it: the board as cubes in one fixed view, seen along one axis and never turned (a folded axis the one seen along, the board the plane it is; a board of three axes seen along z one layer at a time, the layer picked beside the board; the default camera the board's own fit where it shows a Node at six pixels or more and, since 2026-10-21, the lit box on a long board, the Nodes any family lights over the run with the bodies' and the regions' Nodes (the union of the frames' lit boxes, one scale held frame to frame; the frame's own box until that day's visual pass), whole at three pixels per Node or more, the whole board one zoom step out; a dot's least radius --dot-least pixels at the fit's scale beside --dot-floor in Links (2026-10-21); every bar at the credit's floor), the declared board alone in the frame, the layers a receding face grew on the board's own switch, off by default, the frame's label counting the Nodes with a count out there (2026-10-21), every Node at the file's coordinates, the inner faces the world declares as dark cubes with their gaps open ("the faces (declared)" in the legend), one frame per interval, the counts as dots resting on the cubes, the levels as the cubes' glow, a field as mist, the NodeDetectors' Nodes as rings seen at rest and brighter the interval they report; the NodeDetectors' report as the bar chart, the one measurement: with an expectation in the format `tools/click_counts.py` reads one bar per region ordered along the `across` axis, what it saw within the window summed from its `click` lines as click_counts.py sums them and the rounded shares (N times the shares by the largest remainders, the expectation), the dashed blind curve behind them, the pattern's range marked, the totals line and one watch line per key of `watch`; with an expectation of the NodeDetectors' totals one bar per NodeDetector, and with none the totals as reported; the graphs over the intervals beside it, the first the share per family over the lattice in quanta from the look's books per interval, every family's role from the file's row; every number on the page is the files' or the engine's, the page computes nothing but the sums its bars and graphs draw and, where the blind names a line, the discrete transform of one Node's form over the frames, a reading of the drawn trace (2026-10-20), and only the NodeDetectors' report is drawn as a measurement; the blind's numbers in a table of their own, its words under a fold, and with an expectation across an axis the NodeDetectors' arrival over the intervals as two graphs, the reporters' inflow per interval and the quanta gathered within the window, the blind's peak, centroid, span and total marked where it names them; with `--output <world>.output.json` the measurement from the run's own output file (its `click` lines the bars and the arrival, its `credit` lines the clicks credited up to each interval and, since 2026-10-21, where the file carries credit lines at the reporters the bars in the record's own unit W_rec, the clicks the draw credited within the window (the credit lines' counts, the engine's N in that unit) apportioned by the shares of the click lines, with the clicks credited per reporter as a second bar beside them, where the draw fell, and the totals line naming both units (the shares over W_c in T's unit, where one quantum's click read 0), its `density` lines the lattice density over the reporters' regions, the header saying whether the look's click lines are the output's) and with `--reading <reading>.json` (`tools/click_counts.py`'s file) the reading's rounded shares beside the clicks the draw credited and the blind curve, with one table of every row the reader compares, read against blind with the difference; the chart bound by its panel and so by the viewport, `--measure-width` its widest (2026-10-21); `python tools/look/page.py --retemplate <world>.html` writes a recorded page again from its own embedded data (the look, the blind, the output's lines, the reading, a gate's blocks) with the current template and nothing else changed, the way a template fix reaches every shipped page without the engine run again (2026-10-21); the click act from the output file too, the hole (the `lay` lines' Nodes as wire cubes at their interval), the faces presented (the `face` lines' Nodes as small cubes, the hole's and then the front's shells), a record declared a NodeDetector ringed at its standing Nodes and flashing at its absorption, and one panel of the records' clicks (the part each stands in over the intervals from its `credit` lines, the front's sizes taken and distance per interval from the `erasure` lines, the credit lines in a table); the page dark, one theme and no switch, the moving quanta green (light's own pair or the holder of the sign), a cube glowing by the record's form D at its Node; the look is embedded in the page gzip-compressed and base64-encoded and inflated by the browser's own DecompressionStream at load, so that a look of many frames fits one page. A gate's page adds the gate's panels, every number the reader's file's, the blind's or the look's: `PYTHONPATH=src python tools/look/record.py examples/events/ghz/ghz_x_y_y.json` (the look keeps the `parts` lines beside the `click` lines) and, after the GHZ commands of step 6, `PYTHONPATH=src python tools/look/page.py examples/events/ghz/ghz_x_y_y.look.json --blind examples/events/ghz/expectation.json --gate runs/ghz/reading.json --beside examples/events/bell/expectation.json` with `tools/bell_gate.py`'s output written to `runs/ghz/reading.json`, which shows beside the board the input as laid and the run seen from above (the record's counts at its Nodes, each side's region with its declared setting and pattern, the beams meeting the regions, a lattice reading), the `parts` lines per region over the intervals with the parts overlaid (one curve where they are equal) and the region's `click` lines marked, and the reader's credit beside the blind, the 2^n joint shares as bars with the drawn combination (the click) named, E_n, the marginals, the sub-correlations and the local credits of the world in one table, the combination over the gate's worlds (M = -4 on the GHZ, S = 478 / 169 on Bell, each from its reader's file beside its blind) on a ruler from minus to plus the number of its terms with the fence the blind's theorem lines name shaded; the same command on any of the four worlds gives its page; a page without `--gate` holds none of the panels. The variants' three pages under examples/variants/ are no looks: `PYTHONPATH=src python tools/look/line_page.py` writes them from the derivation's scripts' functions and outputs under papers/one_rule/variants/ (2026-10-21), the law's real line and no click, each a canvas player with a slider in the look pages' style, the frames embedded as compact JSON and every number a script's or its output's, the page computing nothing but what it draws.
13. The chain world (2026-10-18, the click's plan, built on the branch claude/click-one-act): `PYTHONPATH=src python examples/events/chain/build_world.py --modes` writes the seven worlds (the six signed permutations of the chain, the images' mode files the original's levels carried by the permutation, and the detuned packet's world with its trials file) and the blind, byte for byte the files there; `PYTHONPATH=src python examples/events/chain/lay_bounds.py` reads the lay's numbers with no interval stepped (the light's unit W_rec and its count, each body's amplitudes, D4's bound per axis and the weight from the unit beside the declared); the blinds are asserted by `tests/test_the_chain_world.py` on every check (2026-10-19, the owner's word, write it as a test): chain_x_minus over its 400 intervals, the inverse over 100, the six worlds over 400 each and the detuned world's eight trials, the three findings by name in the folder's document (the first two resolved in the commit after the test's, the mirror's defect fixed and B7 corrected by derivation). The mirror images and the detuned world left the tree 2026-10-20 at the ninth with their modes and the trials file (CHANGELOG, the ninth), the builder writing the three worlds along the axes; the item's words on the seven worlds are the record of 2026-10-18.
11. ## 7. How to add a family

A family is a row of the universe file, never a line of the engine: its `name`, its `pair`, its `reads` (the holders it reads by name, each at its weight; `{}` for none) and its `dimension`, or for a held family `held` {`sources`, `level_weight`, `write_weight`, optionally `rest` on the massless row of the content and `act` on the holder of the sign}. The rule derives from the pair and the shape what it holds and its lines; its reads are its declaration's and the rows it sources follow by the held write's reciprocity, at the weights it reads with; the engine steps it, reads its share and its currents, holds it and reads it with the code it already has; a charged body of a pair is a second row of that pair at the dimension 2; a record of three real lines is a row at the dimension 3, neutral, laid at its declared `weights` over its lines. The relative part or the centre of two bound records is a row declaring its pair as every family does (the loader derived it from theirs, {`relative_of`: [a, b], `den`} and {`centre_of`: [a, b], `den`}, by `composed_pair` until 2026-10-21, deleted 2026-10-21 with no world, its loader test, its no-root assertions and its two acts-table rows gone with it; ALGEBRA.md, The pair of two bound records). A new act of the law starts from its line in ALGEBRA.md, is a pure function of arrays that calls Rule3 alone, and comes with its test; a new reading of the record is a function of the record's arrays and nothing kept beside them.

`tools/reading_gate.py` (`PYTHONPATH=src python tools/reading_gate.py examples/events/<folder> ...`), the reading gate (the readings are kept in each folder's document and a gate re-runs the gate worlds and compares bit for bit): for every folder named it reads the block `## The gate's table` of the folder's `blind_and_reading.md`, a markdown table of five columns, `label`, `world`, `by`, `reading` and `value`, one row per number read on the tree named in the document (the block is filled for each folder from a run), re-runs the world as the row's `by` says (`run_inputs`, the world once over its `intervals` by `tools/run_inputs.py`, its output file read back;
