## 9. How to build an experiment

The recipe a reader with the repository alone follows to declare a world, write its blind first, run it and read it, derived from the shipped folders of section 4 (the paper's own experiment, `examples/events/two_slits/`, is the pattern; `examples/events/which_way/`, `examples/events/bell/`, `examples/events/ghz/` and `examples/events/matter_alone/` follow it; `examples/events/chain/` writes its blinds before any run, reads the lay's numbers with no interval stepped and asserts the blinds by a test, the pattern for a blind on the click's conservation), from the loader (`src/event_universe/loader/`, `src/event_universe/world_files.py`), the reports (`src/event_universe/reports.py`) and the tools (`tools/`); every key and command below is the code's on `main`. An experiment is one folder under `examples/events/` holding its design (`design.json`), its universe file, its world file with the generator's mode file beside it, its builder (`build_world.py`) and its blind (`expectation.json`); the engine holds no number, so every number of the experiment stands in these files and in nothing else, and the folder is the whole of it.

1. **The universe file**, one JSON object of exactly two keys, `integers` and `families` (`loader/universe.py`, `universe_of`; any other key is refused by name, as everywhere in the files). `integers` holds exactly four, each an integer from 1: `node_clock` (Gamma), `quantum_action` (T), `width` (the bits of the run's integers: at or under 63 every array is the hardware's 64-bit integers, above it Python's, exact and about a hundred times slower) and `link_unit` (G, a power of two, refused otherwise); the shipped files declare 6000, 32768, 63 and 16, and a new universe file copies them (`examples/events/rule.json` is the rule's own). `families` is a list of one row or more, each `name` (its own; a name twice is refused) and `pair` [num, den] with den from 1 and num from -den through den, |num| = den admitted only as num = den, the massless pair (a massive pair has den above |num|: [6000, 6000] is light's, [4000, 6000] matter's, [2400, 2401] the binding holder's, and [-6000, 6000] is refused by name), and then exactly one of two keys: `dimension`, for a family of quanta, any integer from 1: 1 (one real line), 2 (a plane, re and im, whose body the generator lays with a sense, item 2), 3 (three real lines, each stepped as a line of dimension one, the record laid at declared `weights` over them, naming no holder of the sign), the shape by name as a list of the lines' kinds, ["plane", "plane", "plane"] three planes (the proton's row), or the shape [parts, dimension] (that many records of that dimension laid as one event and never summed at a Node: the pair family [2, 1], the GHZ family [4, 1], the deuteron's `nuclide` family [2, 2]; a shape of three numbers and a dimension below 1 are refused); or `held`, for a held row, which declares no dimension: `sources`, exactly one of ["form"] (a holder of the content), ["form", "tensions"] (the massless row of the content with its three axis lines, gravity's) or ["wronskian"] (the holder of the sign, whose own record is light), `level_weight` from 1 (the quanta of form that write one level of the row), `write_weight` from 1 (the write's weight k_w, required, a held row without it refused naming the family), optionally `rest` (the vacuum content, an integer from 0 within the width, admitted on the massless row sourced by the form and refused on the holder of the sign and on a gapped row) and `act`, required (the file states every row's act, none by omission), `pace` (the plain read into the readers' paces) or `rotation` (the turn of the two-part record, admitted on a holder sourced by the Wronskian alone). Who reads whom is the row's own declaration, the key `reads` required on every row (section 4; `loader/derived.py`, `read_of` and `family_rules`; ALGEBRA.md #a-familys-declaration): each holder the family reads by name at the integer weight it reads with, `{}` where it reads none, nothing derived and nothing defaulted, and each family sources what it reads at the same weight, the held write's reciprocity. The amplitude bound A is derived from these numbers and never written (section 4), and a universe at which no level fits inside the width is refused by name. A universe file is read only through a world that names it under `universe`, so its first check is the run of item 4; `examples/events/deuteron.json` is the shortest shipped one, two rows and no holder of the content, the loader's tests' universe with no world on it.

2. **The world file**, one JSON object of at most fourteen keys, `shape`, `boundary`, `face_depth`, `faces`, `intervals`, `universe`, `bodies`, `packets`, `node_detectors`, `receding`, `draw`, `lay`, `erasure` and `uniform`, of which `shape`, `boundary`, `intervals`, `universe`, `bodies` and `node_detectors` are required and `face_depth` with an open axis (`loader/world.py`, `parse_world`; the refusal of a fourteenth key lists the thirteen; CHANGED 2026-10-20, the old kept by name: fourteen keys with `engine`, the engine start file's path, required, left with the ninth commit). The board: `shape` [X, Y, Z], each from 1 (an axis of 1 folds, its two Ports returning the Node itself: the shipped flat worlds declare z 1); `boundary` {`x`, `y`, `z`: each `open`, `periodic` or `closed`}; `face_depth` from 1, the depth of the open faces' reporting layer; optionally `faces`, the inner faces, [{`axis`, `at`, `gaps` [{the other two axes: [first, last]}, ...]}, ...] (the two slits' wall; faces that leave no Node are refused); optionally `receding`, {`x`, `y` or `z`: {`sides` [`low` and/or `high`, each once], `largest` (above the shape's extent on that axis), `layers` (from 1)}}, on an open or closed axis of two Nodes or more (a periodic axis has no face), the board nothing returns from (every shipped world recedes to 400 by 16 layers); `intervals` from 0; `universe`, a repository path from the checkout's root (`engine`, the engine start file `examples/events/engine_start.json`, `{}` since the cleanliness audit of 2026-10-20 and `{"mode": "check"}` before it, left the world file with the ninth commit, 2026-10-20). The lays, two kinds: a body under `bodies`, {`family` (a family of quanta), `nodes` [{`node` [x, y, z], `count` from 1; one Node with the count 1 for a family that reads a holder of the sign, one quantum per charged record}, ...]}, the generator's standing lay: the world declares one Node carrying the whole count, and `tools/pixel_mode.py` finds the body's fixed point in the rows its family reads, rewrites the body's Nodes and counts as the record's share in quanta at the paces of the read (the number the gate reads at the start, so the declaration and the reading are one) and writes its levels to the mode file; a body of a plane is laid rotating in the sense the design file's `senses` names for it, +1 or -1, one per body in the world's order; refused by name: a body whose standing rotation is not above its band's top (a cloud, below the window of mass), a body whose iteration drives a pace to 0 (a collapse, above it) and two bodies whose regions share a Node (`examples/events/matter_alone/pixel.json`); and a packet under `packets`, {`family` (a family of quanta, or a holder of the content for a kick), `along` (`x`, `y` or `z`), `wave` [p, q] (k = pi p / q per Link, p from -q through q and not 0, its sign the direction), `amplitude` (from 1 within A), `top` {`x`, `y`, `z`: [first, last]}, the flat top, and `edge` {`x`, `y`, `z`: the half-width of the raised cosine beyond it, 0 none}, `phase` [r, s] (required; [0, 1] for none) and optionally `transverse` {an axis across the beam: [r, s]}}, the generator's packet lay, a real packet with its level one interval earlier, on a family of several parts laid on every part alike, the symmetric lay (the two slits' one packet over the whole cross-section; the GHZ's record of four parts, laid on every part alike); `whole` is a key the parser knows and refuses by name. The NodeDetectors, under `node_detectors`, each {`name` (its own and not `face`, the loader's own row for the open faces' layer), and either `positions` [[x, y, z], ...], one region of the declared NodeDetector, or `block`, a body's number from 0, whose Nodes report; optionally `basis`, a list of integers not all 0, the reader's setting (p, q), and with it `pattern`, one pair [alpha_k, beta_k] per part of the record of several parts the world lays, none [0, 0]} (`loader/draw.py`; a pattern without a basis, of another length or in a world laying no such record, and a basis on a body's NodeDetector, are refused; the two slits' screen regions declare neither, Bell's and the GHZ's regions both). The size rule (`regions_of_the_law`): a region is two Nodes or more, connected through the Links, and at least q / |p| Nodes across the beam of every packet of its family, on every axis of more than one Node other than the packet's axis, the depth along the beam not gated (a counter for a beam of k = pi / 4 is at least 4 Nodes wide across it; a region of 2 is refused by name with the wavelength). A region's whole, the energy the reader divides its inflow by (the screen's W_c = 3 den T, light's quantum), is no key of the world or of the engine: it stands in the design and in the blind (item 3), and the engine credits every region alike in its record's own unit, read once from the lay (`credit.record_unit`): the family's quantum, largest_below(9 T^2 (den^2 - num^2)) = W_c sin omega_0 for a family with a gap, W_c = 3 den T for a massless one; a count is one quantum of the invariant, 2 A^2 sin omega = T per quantum per line (R8). What a world may not contain: a key the table lacks; a Node outside the shape or beyond an inner face, in a body, in a NodeDetector or as a packet's `whole`; two bodies on one Node; a body or a packet without its entry in the mode file beside the world, or a mode file written for another world (the world's SHA-256 digest stands in it, so a world edited after the generator ran is refused until the generator runs again); a second level pair on a family of dimension 1; a level above A. The generator's command: `PYTHONPATH=src python tools/pixel_mode.py --input <world>.json` (the design file beside the world its inputs, nothing on the command line) writes `<world>.mode.json` beside the world and rewrites the world with the bodies' Nodes and counts; a world of packets alone needs it all the same (`examples/events/two_slits/two_slits.json`), and without it the first refusal a new world meets is the mode file's, before any NodeDetector is checked.

3. **The design file and the blind.** `design.json` holds every number of the experiment and the engine reads none of it: the universe and engine paths, the family, the board's numbers, the lays' numbers, the NodeDetectors with their beams and their wholes, the receding faces, `intervals`, the `window` of intervals the reading runs over, the `seed` of the reader's draw and a `comment` naming the law's lines the blind rests on (the two slits' keys: `universe` and `engine`, `family`, `length` 45 and `height` 48, `wave` [1, 4], `packet` {`column` 10, `edge_along` 6, `across` [4, 44], `edge_across` 2}, `amplitude` 1241 (1328 before commit B, main 7756546d), `wall` 20 with `gaps` [[17, 19], [29, 31]], `screen` 44 and `rows_per_region` 4, `aside` (the bare region `gap`), `receding`, `intervals` 130, `window` [45, 130], `pattern` [1, 10], `spacing` 16, `seed` 24, `draw`, and the blind's rows `blind_per_node`, `blind_through` 273, `arrival` and `wings`; the keys are the builder's, each folder's its own). `build_world.py` beside it reads the design alone and writes the world file, `--folder <folder>` elsewhere and the folder itself by default, with `--modes` calls the generator for the mode file (the two slits' builder always does), and writes `expectation.json` from the design by a formula, before any run and never from a run: a theorem of the law's lines (Bell's S = 478 / 169 by Lagrange's identity on equal parts, the GHZ's M = -4), an exact computation from them (the two slits' Huygens row per region, the bodies' rests and rotations at Gamma 6,000) or a sentence a run answers yes or no; the one number a blind may take from a file is the lay's count from the mode file (`laid`, the generator's reading of the laid record in quanta, no run's); the committed world, mode and expectation files are the builder's byte for byte, so a clean `git status` after the builder is the check that nothing was edited by hand. A blind row is one named entry under `blind` with four keys: `reading` (which lines of the output are read, over which window, by which tool or function), `blind` (the number or the sentence expected), `status` (where it comes from: a theorem, a computation from named lines, nothing read back from a run; where a line it rested on changed, the number as first written is kept beside its restatement under `as_first_written`, `recomputed` and `from`, the families round's folders) and `fence` (the side of the one line the row stands on: `clicks`, answered by the NodeDetectors' `click` and `parts` lines, the measurement, or `Lattice`, answered by a reading of the arrays labelled so, which is never a finding); a `status` and a `fence` also stand on the blind as a whole where its rows are prose (the bodies' looks, `verdict` and `label` "LATTICE"). Two blinds have a reader's format: the screen's (`verdict` "NODEDETECTOR", `node_detector`, `family`, `window`, `across`, `pattern`, `seed`, `counts` per region, `maxima`, `minima`, `central`, `visibility`, `quanta`, `laid`, `arrival`, `wings`, `aside`; `tools/click_counts.py`, section 5) and the gate's (`family`, `window`, `sides`, `settings`, `patterns`, `order`, `runs`, `combination` {`name`, `signs`}, `seed` and `blind` with `correlation`, `marginals`, `S` or `M` and the local credits, each with its `status` and `fence`; `tools/bell_gate.py`); a blind in neither format is read from the output's lines with the readers' functions (item 5). After `expectation.json` is written nothing edits it: a wrong blind stays wrong beside the run's number, and a changed line of the law restates the row with the first number kept.

4. **The run and its output.** At the tag v1.1.0, `PYTHONPATH=src python tools/run_inputs.py --out runs/two_slits examples/events/two_slits/two_slits.json` (several worlds by name, `--jobs 4` in parallel; never a glob over a folder, which takes the mode files, which the runner refuses) prints one line per world, `{"input": "two_slits.json", "verdict": "LAWFUL", "intervals": 130}`, a few seconds on one core, and writes `runs/two_slits/two_slits.output.json`, one JSON object: `input` (the world file's name), `verdict` (`LAWFUL`, or `REFUSED` with `reason`, the loader's, the guard's or the gate's refusal by name at load, or a refusal inside the run with its interval, the lines until then kept), `intervals` (the intervals run, the file's 130 here), `ended` (null here, the board grown to 269 columns of its largest 400 by the end; or the lawful end at a receding face grown to its largest size, {`interval`, `axis`, `side`, `largest`}, the run stopping rather than reflecting), `books` (per family of quanta `share`, `quanta`, `drift`, `pace` and `frozen`, a lattice diagnostic, section 5) and `lines`, every output line in the order written, each `event`, `label`, `interval`, `family`, `node_detector` and its own key: the `click` line (`label` "NODEDETECTOR", `inflow`), the net current into the region through the NodeDetector's front boundary Ports this interval, inward positive, in the current's units, and the `credit` line, the quantum drawn to a region, the one measurement (the two slits' twelve screen regions over the window [45, 130], N credits in all, section 5's numbers; the bare region `gap` none); the `parts` line (`label` "NODEDETECTOR", `levels`), per line of the record of several parts the signed sums [now, before] over the region at the interval's start (the GHZ's four per region; a world of one real line writes none); and the `density` line (`label` "LATTICE", `reading`, or `reading` null with `well` over a frozen Node), the family's density over a declared region where it changed, a lattice reading and no measurement; one line per interval, family and region where the number is not 0, and no line names a Node. What is a click: a `click` line, the NodeDetector's report, with the `parts` line beside it, the NodeDetector's read; what is a lattice reading: a `density` line, the `books`, and every number a tool reads from the arrays (`tools/body_rest.py`, `tools/look/record.py`), each labelled "LATTICE" and never a finding.

5. **The readers and the gates.** The reader the blind's format names, each a host tool holding no number of the law (section 5): `tools/click_counts.py` reads a screen (`examples/events/two_slits/`, `examples/events/which_way/`) by `PYTHONPATH=src python tools/click_counts.py --world <world>.json --output <world>.output.json --expectation <expectation>.json`; `tools/bell_gate.py` reads a gate's four worlds (`examples/events/bell/`, `examples/events/ghz/`) by `PYTHONPATH=src python tools/bell_gate.py --expectation <expectation>.json --outputs <the four output files>`; `tools/body_rest.py` reads the body's look (`examples/events/matter_alone/`), a lattice reading, absorption the worlds by name with `--expectation <expectation>.json` to print the blind beside the reading; every reader prints the blind's numbers beside its own and compares nothing. A blind in no reader's format (the which-way's `rows`; the trials' blinds of the Zeno, the anticoincidence and the resonance folders) is read with the readers' functions (`inflows` of `tools/click_counts.py` sums a region's `click` lines over the window) or by `tools/meeting_trials.py` and the folder's own reader, as its `blind_and_reading.md` says. The gates: `PYTHONPATH=src python tools/back_in_time.py --intervals 40 examples/events/two_slits/two_slits.json` prints `{"verdict": "MATCH", "intervals": 40, "interval": 1, "ended": null}` in a few seconds (section 6, step 5) (MISS names the first interval, array and Node that differ), and a reading is read only on MATCH; `python tools/check.py` before delivery selects the changed files and their consumers (a new folder's `build_world.py` for `ruff`, and the law's tests of `tools/every_pull_request.txt`, `tests/test_documents.py` and `tests/test_repository_language.py` among them, on every selection), `--dry-run` printing the selection and `--full` running the whole suite (section 8). The run's output under `runs/` is never committed.

6. **A worked example**, the two slits' folder as it is on `main`, in ten lines from the checkout's root, each with what it prints:

```bash
cat examples/events/two_slits/design.json        # 1. every number: the 45 x 48 board, the wall at x = 20 with its gaps y 17 to 19 and 29 to 31, the packet at k = pi / 4 from the column 10 at the amplitude 1241, the screen at x = 44 in twelve regions of four rows and the bare region gap, intervals 130, the window [45, 130], the seed 24, the blind's rows
cat examples/events/light.json                   # 2. the universe file: the four integers, the three holders (gravity, the charge, the binding holder) and matter's real line; the two slits' light is the holder of the sign's own record
PYTHONPATH=src python examples/events/two_slits/build_world.py     # 3. writes two_slits.json, two_slits.mode.json (by the generator, which this builder always calls) and the blind expectation.json, byte for byte the files there; git status stays clean
cat examples/events/two_slits/expectation.json   # 4. the blind, written before any run: the Huygens row per region, the pattern's maxima and minima, the laid count 1804 from the mode file, the arrival and the wings with their statuses
PYTHONPATH=src python tools/pixel_mode.py --input examples/events/two_slits/two_slits.json     # 5. the generator alone, after any edit of the world: the mode file beside it, one packet of 1804 quanta (`count`) at the world file's `amplitude` 1241, the mode file's `amplitude` the largest level laid
PYTHONPATH=src python tools/run_inputs.py --out runs/two_slits examples/events/two_slits/two_slits.json      # 6. {"input": "two_slits.json", "verdict": "LAWFUL", "intervals": 130}, a few seconds
PYTHONPATH=src python tools/back_in_time.py --intervals 40 examples/events/two_slits/two_slits.json       # 7. {"verdict": "MATCH", "intervals": 40, "interval": 1, "ended": null}
PYTHONPATH=src python tools/click_counts.py --world examples/events/two_slits/two_slits.json --output runs/two_slits/two_slits.output.json --expectation examples/events/two_slits/expectation.json     # 8. the credits per region over the window beside the blind's row, N the total, the pattern's maxima and minima and the visibility, section 5's numbers
PYTHONPATH=src python tools/look/record.py examples/events/two_slits/two_slits.json --intervals 130 && PYTHONPATH=src python tools/look/page.py examples/events/two_slits/two_slits.look.json --blind examples/events/two_slits/expectation.json     # 9. the one page that shows it, a lattice reading with the NodeDetectors' bars beside the blind curve (section 6, step 10)
python tools/check.py                           # 10. the gates on the changed files before the pull request
```

## 10. Known issues at e5a7feda, and the engine V2 on main with its open items

What the frozen engine leaves by name, none a change of the engine (the decision freezes it; the hash e5a7feda, the merged main, the hash at which the fixes are in (K1 to K6, R339 and R342, A1 to A3, B1 and B3 with C2) and the bug hunt runs; the clean engine is declared when no bug remains).

**The engine V2 on main** (the series of Parts A to H2 over main's fd00a81f5, merged by the branch engine-v2 on the owner's words 6079837569 and 6098936844; what changed from the tag, version by version, in [CHANGELOG.md](../CHANGELOG.md)): Rule3 only. Every act on a level is Rule3's carried division with the remainder kept; everything else is a coefficient's one division or a search by comparison; no shear, no rotation act, no sine or cosine, no root, no float, no table, kept by the three gates of section 8 on every run. What changed from the tag: gravity's odd lines and the sign's Link phase in the fold form (Parts A and B, the pair X^c, X^s per Link with one rounding each, section 3); the time turn replaced by the phase line (Part C, features/phase, `NodeState.phases`, `node.phased`, `plane.link_pairs`; features/rotation deleted); the window's turn by carried divisions on the labels with the hop pair from the phase line (`resonance.window_pair`, `resonance.hopped`); no root anywhere, `rule3.largest_below` at every site and `division_fixed_point` deleted (Part D); the lattice momentum as a reading, the piece's p booked at the detector, the write's twist by bisection on the phase line, the credit line's keys momentum, fan, twist and lost (Parts F and F2); the click with two receivers, the taker's part folded by +p_a and the piece's source's part by -p_a at the click's tick by one twist, the source drawn among the emitters by the quanta each still holds, the credit line's keys source and recoil (Part G; the write's twist and the fold deleted 2026-10-20 on the branch engine-clean, the keys twist and shortfall gone, the readings and the booking standing, this paragraph the record of V2 as merged); the fix pass (Part H): the null window's re-lay keeps each Node's own direction so the fold survives a body's null close, a region taker folds no source and books "P lost to the region" and "unpaid by the region", and the Link's count n_ij carries each end's clock as one coefficient rounding (`paces.turn_factor`, kind D); the file's lay an entry in the emitters' draw by its laid count, `resonance.hopped` without a direction of its own, the acts table's one kind for `phase.iterate` and `gcd` listed as D. The amplitude bounds under the fold: `examples/events/turning.json` 7,591 (5,961 at the tag), `charged.json` 9,266 unchanged, pair_atom 29,482 (the toy world removed on 2026-10-18; 23,302 at the tag); since 2.1.0, the flux line's partial sums, 6,949 and 24,710.
